{"type":"api-request-blob","sessionId":"2ccec162-c0eb-4cb8-a952-47bc94d99a51","hash":"d874de710ae797b80841839789a2482d15f29e12cef37840d64e18dd88740ada","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_0159F1UgfhxG19wR2c2rgyc6","type":"tool_result","content":"\"\"\"SQLite access. One short-lived connection per request.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Iterator\n\nfrom fastapi import Request\n\nSCHEMA = \"\"\"\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (\n    id            INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n    email         TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,\n    password_hash TEXT NOT NULL,\n    role          TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (role IN ('admin', 'editor')),\n    active        INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1\n);\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS posts (\n    id         INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n    title      TEXT NOT NULL,\n    slug       TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,\n    body       TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',\n    status     TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft' CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'published')),\n    author_id  INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES users (id),\n    created_at REAL NOT NULL,\n    updated_at REAL NOT NULL\n);\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef connect(path: Path) -> sqlite3.Connection:\n    conn = sqlite3.connect(path, check_same_thread=False)\n    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n    return conn\n\n\ndef init_db(path: Path) -> None:\n    conn = connect(path)\n    with conn:\n        conn.executescript(SCHEMA)\n    conn.close()\n\n\ndef get_db(request: Request) -> Iterator[sqlite3.Connection]:\n    \"\"\"FastAPI dependency: a connection for this request, closed afterwards.\"\"\"\n    conn = connect(request.app.state.database)\n    try:\n        yield conn\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\"\"\"Posts: Markdown body, Draft/Published status, author, immutable slug.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\nimport sqlite3\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n\nDRAFT = \"draft\"\nPUBLISHED = \"published\"\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass Post:\n    id: int\n    title: str\n    slug: str\n    body: str\n    status: str\n    author_id: int\n    author_email: str\n    created_at: float\n    updated_at: float\n\n\n_SELECT = \"\"\"\nSELECT p.*, u.email AS author_email\nFROM posts p JOIN users u ON u.id = p.author_id\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef _row_to_post(row: sqlite3.Row) -> Post:\n    return Post(row[\"id\"], row[\"title\"], row[\"slug\"], row[\"body\"], row[\"status\"],\n                row[\"author_id\"], row[\"author_email\"], row[\"created_at\"], row[\"updated_at\"])\n\n\ndef slugify(title: str) -> str:\n    return re.sub(r\"[^a-z0-9]+\", \"-\", title.lower()).strip(\"-\") or \"post\"\n\n\ndef _unique_slug(conn: sqlite3.Connection, title: str) -> str:\n    base = slugify(title)\n    slug, n = base, 1\n    while conn.execute(\"SELECT 1 FROM posts WHERE slug = ?\", (slug,)).fetchone():\n        n += 1\n        slug = f\"{base}-{n}\"\n    return slug\n\n\ndef create_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, title: str, body: str, author_id: int,\n                now: float) -> int:\n    \"\"\"New Posts are always Drafts.\"\"\"\n    with conn:\n        cur = conn.execute(\n            \"INSERT INTO posts (title, slug, body, status, author_id, created_at, updated_at)\"\n            \" VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n            (title, _unique_slug(conn, title), body, DRAFT, author_id, now, now),\n        )\n    return cur.lastrowid\n\n\ndef get_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post_id: int) -> Post | None:\n    row = conn.execute(_SELECT + \" WHERE p.id = ?\", (post_id,)).fetchone()\n    return _row_to_post(row) if row else None\n\n\ndef list_posts(conn: sqlite3.Connection, author_id: int | None = None,\n               status: str | None = None) -> list[Post]:\n    \"\"\"Newest first. author_id limits the list to one User's Posts, status to one status.\"\"\"\n    clauses, args = [], []\n    if author_id is not None:\n        clauses.append(\"p.author_id = ?\")\n        args.append(author_id)\n    if status is not None:\n        clauses.append(\"p.status = ?\")\n        args.append(status)\n    where = \" WHERE \" + \" AND \".join(clauses) if clauses else \"\"\n    rows = conn.execute(_SELECT + where + \" ORDER BY p.created_at DESC, p.id DESC\",\n                        args).fetchall()\n    return [_row_to_post(r) for r in rows]\n\n\ndef count_by_status(conn: sqlite3.Connection, author_id: int | None = None) -> dict[str, int]:\n    \"\"\"Posts per status (draft, published), optionally for one author.\"\"\"\n    sql, args = \"SELECT status, COUNT(*) AS n FROM posts\", ()\n    if author_id is not None:\n        sql, args = sql + \" WHERE author_id = ?\", (author_id,)\n    found = {r[\"status\"]: r[\"n\"] for r in conn.execute(sql + \" GROUP BY status\", args)}\n    return {DRAFT: found.get(DRAFT, 0), PUBLISHED: found.get(PUBLISHED, 0)}\n\n\ndef update_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post_id: int, title: str, body: str,\n                now: float, status: str, slug: str | None = None) -> None:\n    \"\"\"The slug changes only when a normalised `slug` is passed (Admin-only callers).\"\"\"\n    with conn:\n        conn.execute(\n            \"UPDATE posts SET title = ?, body = ?, status = ?, updated_at = ?,\"\n            \" slug = COALESCE(?, slug) WHERE id = ?\",\n            (title, body, status, now, slug, post_id),\n        )\n\n\ndef set_status(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post_id: int, status: str, now: float) -> None:\n    with conn:\n        conn.execute(\"UPDATE posts SET status = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?\",\n                     (status, now, post_id))\n\n\ndef slug_taken(conn: sqlite3.Connection, slug: str, post_id: int) -> bool:\n    return conn.execute(\"SELECT 1 FROM posts WHERE slug = ? AND id != ?\",\n                        (slug, post_id)).fetchone() is not None\n\n\ndef delete_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post_id: int) -> None:\n    with conn:\n        conn.execute(\"DELETE FROM posts WHERE id = ?\", (post_id,))\n\"\"\"Console routes for Posts: list, create, edit, preview, delete.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Form, HTTPException, Query, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\n\nfrom app import auth, db, posts\nfrom app.markdown import render_markdown\nfrom app.templating import templates\n\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/admin/posts\")\n\nNOT_YOURS = \"You can only open your own Posts\"\n\n\ndef _own_post(conn, post_id: int, user) -> posts.Post:\n    \"\"\"The Post, or a refusal. Editors reach only their own; Admins reach all.\"\"\"\n    post = posts.get_post(conn, post_id)\n    if post is None:\n        # An Editor gets the same refusal as for someone else's Post, so ids do not leak.\n        if user.role != \"admin\":\n            raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=NOT_YOURS)\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=\"No such Post\")\n    if user.role != \"admin\" and post.author_id != user.id:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=NOT_YOURS)\n    return post\n\n\ndef _editor_page(request, user, post, title, body, error=None, status_code=200):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/post_edit.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Edit Post\" if post else \"New Post\", \"user\": user, \"post\": post,\n         \"form_title\": title, \"form_body\": body, \"preview_html\": render_markdown(body),\n         \"error\": error},\n        status_code=status_code,\n    )\n\n\nSTATUS_FILTERS = {\"all\": None, \"published\": posts.PUBLISHED, \"draft\": posts.DRAFT}\nTYPE_FILTERS = (\"all\", \"post\", \"page\")\n\n\n@router.get(\"\")\ndef post_list(request: Request, status: str = \"all\", kind: str = Query(\"all\", alias=\"type\"),\n              user=Depends(auth.require_user), conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    \"\"\"Content list. Unknown filter values fall back to All.\"\"\"\n    status = status if status in STATUS_FILTERS else \"all\"\n    kind = kind if kind in TYPE_FILTERS else \"all\"\n    own_only = None if user.role == \"admin\" else user.id\n    # Pages arrive in T05; until then the Page filter shows an empty list.\n    shows_posts = kind != \"page\"\n    counts = posts.count_by_status(conn, own_only) if shows_posts else {}\n    shown = posts.list_posts(conn, own_only, STATUS_FILTERS[status]) if shows_posts else []\n    published, drafts = counts.get(posts.PUBLISHED, 0), counts.get(posts.DRAFT, 0)\n    status_links = [(\"all\", \"All\", published + drafts), (\"published\", \"Published\", published),\n                    (\"draft\", \"Draft\", drafts)]\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/post_list.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Content list\", \"user\": user, \"posts\": shown, \"status\": status, \"type\": kind,\n         \"status_links\": status_links, \"type_links\": TYPE_FILTERS if user.role == \"admin\" else ()},\n    )\n\n\n@router.get(\"/new\")\ndef post_new(request: Request, user=Depends(auth.require_user)):\n    return _editor_page(request, user, None, \"\", \"\")\n\n\n@router.post(\"\")\ndef post_create(request: Request, title: str = Form(\"\"), body: str = Form(\"\"),\n                user=Depends(auth.require_user), conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    title = title.strip()\n    if not title:\n        return _editor_page(request, user, None, title, body, \"A Post needs a title\", 400)\n    post_id = posts.create_post(conn, title, body, user.id, auth.now(request))\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/preview\")\ndef post_preview(request: Request, title: str = Form(\"\"), body: str = Form(\"\"),\n                 post_id: int | None = Form(None),\n                 user=Depends(auth.require_user), conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    post = _own_post(conn, post_id, user) if post_id else None\n    return _editor_page(request, user, post, title, body)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{post_id}\")\ndef post_edit(post_id: int, request: Request, user=Depends(auth.require_user),\n              conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    post = _own_post(conn, post_id, user)\n    return _editor_page(request, user, post, post.title, post.body)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{post_id}\")\ndef post_update(post_id: int, request: Request, title: str = Form(\"\"), body: str = Form(\"\"),\n                slug: str | None = Form(None), user=Depends(auth.require_user), conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    post = _own_post(conn, post_id, user)\n    title = title.strip()\n    if not title:\n        return _editor_page(request, user, post, title, body, \"A Post needs a title\", 400)\n    new_slug = None\n    if user.role == \"admin\" and slug is not None and slug.strip():\n        new_slug = posts.slugify(slug)\n        if posts.slug_taken(conn, new_slug, post_id):\n            return _editor_page(request, user, post, title, body,\n                                f\"The slug “{new_slug}” is already used by another Post\", 400)\n    # An Editor's edit sends a Published Post back to Draft; an Admin's leaves it alone.\n    status = post.status if user.role == \"admin\" else posts.DRAFT\n    posts.update_post(conn, post_id, title, body, auth.now(request), status, new_slug)\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\ndef _deletable(post: posts.Post, user) -> posts.Post:\n    if user.role != \"admin\" and post.status != posts.DRAFT:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=\"Only a President can delete a Published Post\")\n    return post\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{post_id}/delete\")\ndef post_delete_confirm(post_id: int, request: Request, user=Depends(auth.require_user),\n                        conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    post = _deletable(_own_post(conn, post_id, user), user)\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/post_delete.html\", {\"title\": \"Delete Post\", \"user\": user, \"post\": post}\n    )\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{post_id}/delete\")\ndef post_delete(post_id: int, user=Depends(auth.require_user), conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    post = _deletable(_own_post(conn, post_id, user), user)\n    posts.delete_post(conn, post.id)\n    return RedirectResponse(\"/admin/posts\", status_code=303)\n\n\nCONFIRMS = {\n    \"publish\": (\"Publish this?\", \"Yes, publish it\", posts.DRAFT, posts.PUBLISHED,\n                \"It becomes Live at the next Publish run.\"),\n    \"unpublish\": (\"Are you sure you want to unpublish this?\", \"Yes, unpublish it\",\n                  posts.PUBLISHED, posts.DRAFT,\n                  \"It returns to Draft and leaves the public site at the next Publish run.\"),\n}\n\nadmin_only = Depends(auth.require_role(\"admin\"))\n\n\ndef _confirm_page(action: str, post_id: int, request, user, conn):\n    heading, button, _, _, note = CONFIRMS[action]\n    post = _own_post(conn, post_id, user)\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/post_confirm.html\",\n        {\"title\": heading, \"user\": user, \"post\": post, \"action\": action,\n         \"heading\": heading, \"button\": button, \"note\": note},\n    )\n\n\ndef _change_status(action: str, post_id: int, request, user, conn):\n    _, _, from_status, to_status, _ = CONFIRMS[action]\n    post = _own_post(conn, post_id, user)\n    if post.status == from_status:\n        posts.set_status(conn, post_id, to_status, auth.now(request))\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{post_id}/publish\")\ndef post_publish_confirm(post_id: int, request: Request, user=admin_only,\n                         conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    return _confirm_page(\"publish\", post_id, request, user, conn)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{post_id}/publish\")\ndef post_publish(post_id: int, request: Request, user=admin_only, conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    return _change_status(\"publish\", post_id, request, user, conn)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{post_id}/unpublish\")\ndef post_unpublish_confirm(post_id: int, request: Request, user=admin_only,\n                           conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    return _confirm_page(\"unpublish\", post_id, request, user, conn)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{post_id}/unpublish\")\ndef post_unpublish(post_id: int, request: Request, user=admin_only, conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    return _change_status(\"unpublish\", post_id, request, user, conn)\n\"\"\"The admin console's home page and the /admin catch-all.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Request\n\nfrom app import auth, db, posts, users\nfrom app.templating import templates\n\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/admin\")\n\n# Include this router LAST: an unknown /admin address still sends anonymous\n# visitors to the login page instead of a bare 404.\ncatch_all = APIRouter(prefix=\"/admin\")\n\n\n@router.get(\"\")\ndef admin_home(request: Request, user=Depends(auth.require_user), conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    is_admin = user.role == \"admin\"\n    counts = posts.count_by_status(conn, None if is_admin else user.id)\n    active, deactivated = users.count_by_active(conn) if is_admin else (None, None)\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/hello.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Admin\", \"user\": user, \"drafts\": counts[posts.DRAFT],\n         \"published\": counts[posts.PUBLISHED], \"active_users\": active,\n         \"deactivated_users\": deactivated},\n    )\n\n\n@catch_all.api_route(\"/{rest:path}\", include_in_schema=False,\n                     methods=[\"GET\", \"POST\", \"PUT\", \"PATCH\", \"DELETE\"],\n                     dependencies=[Depends(auth.require_user)])\ndef admin_unknown(rest: str):\n    raise HTTPException(status_code=404)\n\"\"\"Create the demo President and Officer. Safe to run repeatedly.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom app import db, users\n\n\ndef seed_users(database: Path, accounts: dict[str, tuple[str, str]]) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"accounts maps role -> (email, password). Existing emails are left alone.\n\n    Returns the emails that were created. Passwords are hashed by users.create_user\n    and are never printed or stored as text.\n    \"\"\"\n    db.init_db(database)\n    conn = db.connect(database)\n    created = []\n    for role, (email, password) in accounts.items():\n        if not users.email_exists(conn, email):\n            users.create_user(conn, email, password, role)\n            created.append(email)\n    conn.close()\n    return created\n\"\"\"The FastAPI application.\n\nThe admin console answers at /admin and the public site answers at /. Sign-in,\nsessions, CSRF and the permission guard live in app/auth.py; add routes in their\nown modules (app/routes/...) and include them here. Keep this file small.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport time\nfrom contextlib import asynccontextmanager\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Callable\n\nfrom fastapi import Depends, FastAPI, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\nfrom starlette.middleware.sessions import SessionMiddleware\n\nfrom app import auth, db, settings\nfrom app.routes import admin as admin_routes\nfrom app.routes import auth as auth_routes\nfrom app.routes import posts as post_routes\nfrom app.templating import templates\n\n\ndef create_app(database: Path | None = None,\n               clock: Callable[[], float] | None = None) -> FastAPI:\n    settings.require_safe_secret_key()\n    database = Path(database or settings.DATABASE_PATH)\n\n    @asynccontextmanager\n    async def lifespan(_: FastAPI):\n        db.init_db(database)\n        yield\n\n    app = FastAPI(title=\"IPHS 400 MP2 CMS\", lifespan=lifespan,\n                  dependencies=[Depends(auth.csrf_protect)])\n    app.state.database = database\n    app.state.clock = clock or time.time\n    app.add_middleware(SessionMiddleware, secret_key=settings.SECRET_KEY,\n                       session_cookie=\"cms_session\", same_site=\"lax\",\n                       max_age=None)\n\n    @app.exception_handler(auth.NotSignedIn)\n    async def to_login(request: Request, exc: auth.NotSignedIn):\n        return RedirectResponse(\"/login\", status_code=303)\n\n    app.include_router(auth_routes.router)\n    app.include_router(admin_routes.router)\n    app.include_router(post_routes.router)\n\n    @app.get(\"/\")\n    def public_home(request: Request):\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\n            request, \"public/home.html\",\n            {\"title\": settings.SITE_TITLE, \"items\": []},\n        )\n\n    # Later ticket routers plug in here, above the catch-all.\n    app.include_router(admin_routes.catch_all)  # keep LAST\n\n    return app\n\n\napp = create_app()\n\"\"\"Sessions, CSRF and the permission guard. Every admin route goes through here.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport secrets\n\nfrom fastapi import Depends, HTTPException, Request\nfrom jinja2 import pass_context\n\nfrom app import db, settings, users\n\nSAFE_METHODS = {\"GET\", \"HEAD\", \"OPTIONS\"}\n\n\nclass NotSignedIn(Exception):\n    \"\"\"Raised by the guard; the app turns it into a redirect to /login.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef ensure_csrf(request: Request) -> str:\n    token = request.session.get(\"csrf\")\n    if not token:\n        token = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)\n        request.session[\"csrf\"] = token\n    return token\n\n\n@pass_context\ndef csrf_token(context) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Use as {{ csrf_token() }} inside every state-changing form.\"\"\"\n    return ensure_csrf(context[\"request\"])\n\n\nasync def csrf_protect(request: Request) -> None:\n    \"\"\"App-wide: any request that is not GET/HEAD/OPTIONS needs a valid token.\"\"\"\n    if request.method in SAFE_METHODS:\n        return\n    if request.url.path.startswith(\"/admin\") and \"user_id\" not in request.session:\n        raise NotSignedIn  # anonymous: send to login rather than a CSRF error\n    form = await request.form()\n    sent = str(form.get(\"csrf_token\", \"\"))\n    expected = request.session.get(\"csrf\", \"\")\n    if not expected or not secrets.compare_digest(sent, expected):\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=\"Invalid or missing CSRF token\")\n\n\ndef now(request: Request) -> float:\n    return request.app.state.clock()\n\n\ndef start_session(request: Request, user: users.User) -> None:\n    request.session.clear()  # fresh session on sign-in\n    request.session[\"user_id\"] = user.id\n    request.session[\"last_seen\"] = now(request)\n    ensure_csrf(request)\n\n\ndef end_session(request: Request) -> None:\n    request.session.clear()\n\n\ndef require_user(request: Request, conn=Depends(db.get_db)) -> users.User:\n    \"\"\"Signed in, not idle too long, and still active; otherwise to the login page.\"\"\"\n    user_id = request.session.get(\"user_id\")\n    last_seen = request.session.get(\"last_seen\")\n    current = now(request)\n    if user_id is None or last_seen is None or current - last_seen > settings.IDLE_TIMEOUT_SECONDS:\n        end_session(request)\n        raise NotSignedIn\n    user = users.get_user(conn, user_id)\n    if user is None or not user.active:\n        end_session(request)\n        raise NotSignedIn\n    request.session[\"last_seen\"] = current\n    return user\n\n\ndef require_role(*roles: str):\n    \"\"\"Guard for role-restricted routes: Depends(require_role(\"admin\")).\"\"\"\n\n    def guard(user: users.User = Depends(require_user)) -> users.User:\n        if user.role not in roles:\n            raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=\"You do not have access to this page\")\n        return user\n\n    return guard\n\"\"\"Shared test fixtures.\n\n`client` gives you the app. `client_as(role)` gives you a client that is logged\nin as a seeded user of that role — it works as soon as your login route exists,\nso access-control tests stay one line:\n\n    def test_editor_cannot_manage_users(client_as):\n        assert client_as(\"editor\").get(\"/admin/users\").status_code in (302, 403)\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\n\nimport pytest\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n\nfrom app.main import create_app\n\n# Matches scripts/seed_demo.py. Passwords come from the environment there; in\n# tests they are fixed and meaningless.\nDEMO_USERS = {\n    \"admin\": {\"email\": \"admin@example.test\", \"password\": \"test-admin-pw\"},\n    \"editor\": {\"email\": \"editor@example.test\", \"password\": \"test-editor-pw\"},\n}\n\n\nclass FakeClock:\n    \"\"\"Injected clock so idle sign-out is tested without waiting.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, now: float = 1_000_000.0):\n        self.now = now\n\n    def __call__(self) -> float:\n        return self.now\n\n    def advance(self, seconds: float) -> None:\n        self.now += seconds\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef clock() -> FakeClock:\n    return FakeClock()\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef make_app(tmp_path, clock):\n    \"\"\"Factory for a fresh app on its own seeded database and the fake clock.\"\"\"\n    from app.seed import seed_users\n\n    def _make():\n        db = tmp_path / \"test.db\"\n        application = create_app(database=db, clock=clock)\n        seed_users(db, {role: (u[\"email\"], u[\"password\"])\n                        for role, u in DEMO_USERS.items()})\n        return application\n\n    return _make\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef client(make_app) -> TestClient:\n    return TestClient(make_app())\n\n\ndef csrf_of(client: TestClient, path: str = \"/login\") -> str:\n    \"\"\"Read the CSRF token out of the form on `path`.\"\"\"\n    html = client.get(path).text\n    return re.search(r'name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"([^\"]+)\"', html).group(1)\n\n\ndef sign_in(client: TestClient, email: str, password: str, token: str | None = None):\n    return client.post(\n        \"/login\",\n        data={\"email\": email, \"password\": password,\n              \"csrf_token\": token or csrf_of(client)},\n        follow_redirects=False,\n    )\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef client_as(make_app):\n    \"\"\"Return a factory: client_as(\"editor\") -> a logged-in TestClient.\"\"\"\n\n    def _login(role: str) -> TestClient:\n        user = DEMO_USERS[role]\n        c = TestClient(make_app())\n        response = sign_in(c, user[\"email\"], user[\"password\"])\n        assert response.status_code in (200, 302, 303), (\n            f\"Login as {role} failed with {response.status_code}\")\n        return c\n\n    return _login","is_error":false},{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01D4vVHiqVaDHiGzufyn2GS1","type":"tool_result","content":"<!doctype html>\n<html lang=\"en\">\n<head>\n  <meta charset=\"utf-8\">\n  <meta name=\"viewport\" content=\"width=device-width, initial-scale=1\">\n  <title>{% block title %}{{ title }}{% endblock %}</title>\n  <link rel=\"stylesheet\" href=\"{{ css_path | default('style.css') }}\">\n</head>\n<body>\n  <header><a href=\"{{ home_path | default('index.html') }}\">{{ title }}</a></header>\n  <main>{% block content %}{% endblock %}</main>\n  <footer><small>IPHS 400 Mini-Project #2</small></footer>\n</body>\n</html>\n{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>Content list</h1>\n  <p><a href=\"/admin/posts/new\">New Post</a> · <a href=\"/admin\">Dashboard</a></p>\n  <nav aria-label=\"Status\">\n    {% for key, label, n in status_links %}\n    <a href=\"/admin/posts?status={{ key }}&amp;type={{ type }}\">{{ label }} ({{ n }})</a>{% if not loop.last %} · {% endif %}\n    {% endfor %}\n  </nav>\n  <nav aria-label=\"Type\">\n    {% for key in type_links %}\n    <a href=\"/admin/posts?status={{ status }}&amp;type={{ key }}\">{{ key | capitalize }}</a>{% if not loop.last %} · {% endif %}\n    {% endfor %}\n  </nav>\n  {% if posts %}\n  <ul>\n    {% for post in posts %}\n    <li>\n      <a href=\"/admin/posts/{{ post.id }}\">{{ post.title }}</a>\n      — {{ post.status | capitalize }}, by {{ post.author_email }}\n    </li>\n    {% endfor %}\n  </ul>\n  {% else %}\n  <p>Nothing here yet.</p>\n  {% endif %}\n{% endblock %}\n{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>{{ title }}</h1>\n  <p><a href=\"/admin/posts\">All Posts</a></p>\n  {% if error %}<p role=\"alert\">{{ error }}</p>{% endif %}\n  <div style=\"display: flex; gap: 1rem; flex-wrap: wrap;\">\n    <form method=\"post\" action=\"{{ '/admin/posts/' ~ post.id if post else '/admin/posts' }}\"\n          style=\"flex: 1 1 20rem;\">\n      <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token() }}\">\n      {% if post %}<input type=\"hidden\" name=\"post_id\" value=\"{{ post.id }}\">{% endif %}\n      <p><label>Title<br>\n        <input type=\"text\" name=\"title\" value=\"{{ form_title }}\" required style=\"width: 100%;\"></label></p>\n      {% if post %}\n      {% if user.role == 'admin' %}\n      <p><label>Slug<br>\n        <input type=\"text\" name=\"slug\" value=\"{{ post.slug }}\" style=\"width: 100%;\"></label></p>\n      {% else %}\n      <p>Slug: <code>{{ post.slug }}</code> (set when first saved)</p>\n      {% endif %}\n      <p>Status: {{ post.status | capitalize }}</p>\n      {% endif %}\n      <p><label>Body (Markdown)<br>\n        <textarea name=\"body\" rows=\"14\" style=\"width: 100%;\">{{ form_body }}</textarea></label></p>\n      <p>\n        <button type=\"submit\">Save Draft</button>\n        <button type=\"submit\" formaction=\"/admin/posts/preview\">Preview</button>\n      </p>\n    </form>\n    <section style=\"flex: 1 1 20rem;\" aria-label=\"Preview\">\n      <h2>Preview</h2>\n      {% if not post or post.status == 'draft' %}\n      <p><strong>Draft, not Live</strong></p>\n      {% else %}\n      <p><strong>Published</strong> (Live after the next Publish run)</p>\n      {% endif %}\n      {{ preview_html | safe }}\n    </section>\n  </div>\n  {% if post %}\n  <p>\n    {% if user.role == 'admin' %}\n      {% if post.status == 'draft' %}<a href=\"/admin/posts/{{ post.id }}/publish\">Publish</a> ·\n      {% else %}<a href=\"/admin/posts/{{ post.id }}/unpublish\">Unpublish</a> ·{% endif %}\n    {% endif %}\n    {% if user.role == 'admin' or post.status == 'draft' %}\n    <a href=\"/admin/posts/{{ post.id }}/delete\">Delete this Post</a>\n    {% endif %}\n  </p>\n  {% endif %}\n{% endblock %}\n{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>{{ heading }}</h1>\n  <p>“{{ post.title }}” — {{ note }}</p>\n  <form method=\"post\" action=\"/admin/posts/{{ post.id }}/{{ action }}\">\n    <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token() }}\">\n    <button type=\"submit\">{{ button }}</button>\n    <a href=\"/admin/posts/{{ post.id }}\">Cancel</a>\n  </form>\n{% endblock %}\n{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>Delete this Post?</h1>\n  <p>Are you sure you want to permanently delete “{{ post.title }}”? This cannot be undone.</p>\n  <form method=\"post\" action=\"/admin/posts/{{ post.id }}/delete\">\n    <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token() }}\">\n    <button type=\"submit\">Yes, delete it</button>\n    <a href=\"/admin/posts/{{ post.id }}\">Cancel</a>\n  </form>\n{% endblock %}\n\"\"\"T04: Dashboard counts and the filterable Content list. Seam 1: the HTTP app.\"\"\"\nimport re\n\nfrom app import db\nfrom tests.test_t02_posts import create_post, post_url\nfrom tests.test_t03_president_control import act, president_for\n\n\ndef count_of(html: str, label: str) -> int:\n    \"\"\"The number printed next to a label such as 'Drafts: 3'.\"\"\"\n    return int(re.search(rf\"{label}:\\s*(\\d+)\", html).group(1))\n\n\ndef titles_in_order(html: str, titles: list[str]) -> list[str]:\n    return sorted((t for t in titles if t in html), key=html.index)\n\n\ndef test_dashboard_counts_follow_post_changes(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    admin = president_for(editor)\n    assert count_of(admin.get(\"/admin\").text, \"Drafts\") == 0\n    url = post_url(create_post(editor, title=\"One\"))\n    create_post(editor, title=\"Two\")\n    page = admin.get(\"/admin\").text\n    assert (count_of(page, \"Drafts\"), count_of(page, \"Published\")) == (2, 0)\n    act(admin, url, \"publish\")\n    page = admin.get(\"/admin\").text\n    assert (count_of(page, \"Drafts\"), count_of(page, \"Published\")) == (1, 1)\n\n\ndef test_dashboard_has_a_new_post_button_that_opens_the_editor(client_as):\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    page = c.get(\"/admin\").text\n    assert 'href=\"/admin/posts/new\"' in page\n    assert c.get(\"/admin/posts/new\").status_code == 200\n\n\ndef test_president_sees_user_counts_officer_does_not(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    admin = president_for(editor)\n    page = admin.get(\"/admin\").text\n    assert (count_of(page, \"Active Users\"), count_of(page, \"Deactivated Users\")) == (2, 0)\n    conn = db.connect(editor.app.state.database)\n    with conn:\n        conn.execute(\"UPDATE users SET active = 0 WHERE role = 'editor'\")\n    conn.close()\n    page = admin.get(\"/admin\").text\n    assert (count_of(page, \"Active Users\"), count_of(page, \"Deactivated Users\")) == (1, 1)\n    assert \"Active Users\" not in editor.get(\"/admin\").text\n    assert \"Deactivated Users\" not in editor.get(\"/admin\").text\n\n\ndef test_content_list_is_newest_first_with_status_links_and_counts(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    admin = president_for(editor)\n    old = post_url(create_post(editor, title=\"Older post\"))\n    create_post(editor, title=\"Newer post\")\n    act(admin, old, \"publish\")\n    page = admin.get(\"/admin/posts\").text\n    assert titles_in_order(page, [\"Older post\", \"Newer post\"]) == [\"Newer post\", \"Older post\"]","is_error":false}]}}

{"parentUuid":"757b0cfa-37ff-4410-943c-3ce06009c48a","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"c231bb73-8d53-4531-b686-e572c28d9639","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_0159F1UgfhxG19wR2c2rgyc6","type":"tool_result","content":"\"\"\"SQLite access. One short-lived connection per request.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Iterator\n\nfrom fastapi import Request\n\nSCHEMA = \"\"\"\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (\n    id            INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n    email         TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,\n    password_hash TEXT NOT NULL,\n    role          TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (role IN ('admin', 'editor')),\n    active        INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1\n);\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS posts (\n    id         INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n    title      TEXT NOT NULL,\n    slug       TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,\n    body       TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',\n    status     TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft' CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'published')),\n    author_id  INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES users (id),\n    created_at REAL NOT NULL,\n    updated_at REAL NOT NULL\n);\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef connect(path: Path) -> sqlite3.Connection:\n    conn = sqlite3.connect(path, check_same_thread=False)\n    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n    return conn\n\n\ndef init_db(path: Path) -> None:\n    conn = connect(path)\n    with conn:\n        conn.executescript(SCHEMA)\n    conn.close()\n\n\ndef get_db(request: Request) -> Iterator[sqlite3.Connection]:\n    \"\"\"FastAPI dependency: a connection for this request, closed afterwards.\"\"\"\n    conn = connect(request.app.state.database)\n    try:\n        yield conn\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\"\"\"Posts: Markdown body, Draft/Published status, author, immutable slug.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\nimport sqlite3\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n\nDRAFT = \"draft\"\nPUBLISHED = \"published\"\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass Post:\n    id: int\n    title: str\n    slug: str\n    body: str\n    status: str\n    author_id: int\n    author_email: str\n    created_at: float\n    updated_at: float\n\n\n_SELECT = \"\"\"\nSELECT p.*, u.email AS author_email\nFROM posts p JOIN users u ON u.id = p.author_id\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef _row_to_post(row: sqlite3.Row) -> Post:\n    return Post(row[\"id\"], row[\"title\"], row[\"slug\"], row[\"body\"], row[\"status\"],\n                row[\"author_id\"], row[\"author_email\"], row[\"created_at\"], row[\"updated_at\"])\n\n\ndef slugify(title: str) -> str:\n    return re.sub(r\"[^a-z0-9]+\", \"-\", title.lower()).strip(\"-\") or \"post\"\n\n\ndef _unique_slug(conn: sqlite3.Connection, title: str) -> str:\n    base = slugify(title)\n    slug, n = base, 1\n    while conn.execute(\"SELECT 1 FROM posts WHERE slug = ?\", (slug,)).fetchone():\n        n += 1\n        slug = f\"{base}-{n}\"\n    return slug\n\n\ndef create_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, title: str, body: str, author_id: int,\n                now: float) -> int:\n    \"\"\"New Posts are always Drafts.\"\"\"\n    with conn:\n        cur = conn.execute(\n            \"INSERT INTO posts (title, slug, body, status, author_id, created_at, updated_at)\"\n            \" VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n            (title, _unique_slug(conn, title), body, DRAFT, author_id, now, now),\n        )\n    return cur.lastrowid\n\n\ndef get_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post_id: int) -> Post | None:\n    row = conn.execute(_SELECT + \" WHERE p.id = ?\", (post_id,)).fetchone()\n    return _row_to_post(row) if row else None\n\n\ndef list_posts(conn: sqlite3.Connection, author_id: int | None = None,\n               status: str | None = None) -> list[Post]:\n    \"\"\"Newest first. author_id limits the list to one User's Posts, status to one status.\"\"\"\n    clauses, args = [], []\n    if author_id is not None:\n        clauses.append(\"p.author_id = ?\")\n        args.append(author_id)\n    if status is not None:\n        clauses.append(\"p.status = ?\")\n        args.append(status)\n    where = \" WHERE \" + \" AND \".join(clauses) if clauses else \"\"\n    rows = conn.execute(_SELECT + where + \" ORDER BY p.created_at DESC, p.id DESC\",\n                        args).fetchall()\n    return [_row_to_post(r) for r in rows]\n\n\ndef count_by_status(conn: sqlite3.Connection, author_id: int | None = None) -> dict[str, int]:\n    \"\"\"Posts per status (draft, published), optionally for one author.\"\"\"\n    sql, args = \"SELECT status, COUNT(*) AS n FROM posts\", ()\n    if author_id is not None:\n        sql, args = sql + \" WHERE author_id = ?\", (author_id,)\n    found = {r[\"status\"]: r[\"n\"] for r in conn.execute(sql + \" GROUP BY status\", args)}\n    return {DRAFT: found.get(DRAFT, 0), PUBLISHED: found.get(PUBLISHED, 0)}\n\n\ndef update_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post_id: int, title: str, body: str,\n                now: float, status: str, slug: str | None = None) -> None:\n    \"\"\"The slug changes only when a normalised `slug` is passed (Admin-only callers).\"\"\"\n    with conn:\n        conn.execute(\n            \"UPDATE posts SET title = ?, body = ?, status = ?, updated_at = ?,\"\n            \" slug = COALESCE(?, slug) WHERE id = ?\",\n            (title, body, status, now, slug, post_id),\n        )\n\n\ndef set_status(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post_id: int, status: str, now: float) -> None:\n    with conn:\n        conn.execute(\"UPDATE posts SET status = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?\",\n                     (status, now, post_id))\n\n\ndef slug_taken(conn: sqlite3.Connection, slug: str, post_id: int) -> bool:\n    return conn.execute(\"SELECT 1 FROM posts WHERE slug = ? AND id != ?\",\n                        (slug, post_id)).fetchone() is not None\n\n\ndef delete_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post_id: int) -> None:\n    with conn:\n        conn.execute(\"DELETE FROM posts WHERE id = ?\", (post_id,))\n\"\"\"Console routes for Posts: list, create, edit, preview, delete.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Form, HTTPException, Query, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\n\nfrom app import auth, db, posts\nfrom app.markdown import render_markdown\nfrom app.templating import templates\n\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/admin/posts\")\n\nNOT_YOURS = \"You can only open your own Posts\"\n\n\ndef _own_post(conn, post_id: int, user) -> posts.Post:\n    \"\"\"The Post, or a refusal. Editors reach only their own; Admins reach all.\"\"\"\n    post = posts.get_post(conn, post_id)\n    if post is None:\n        # An Editor gets the same refusal as for someone else's Post, so ids do not leak.\n        if user.role != \"admin\":\n            raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=NOT_YOURS)\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=\"No such Post\")\n    if user.role != \"admin\" and post.author_id != user.id:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=NOT_YOURS)\n    return post\n\n\ndef _editor_page(request, user, post, title, body, error=None, status_code=200):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/post_edit.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Edit Post\" if post else \"New Post\", \"user\": user, \"post\": post,\n         \"form_title\": title, \"form_body\": body, \"preview_html\": render_markdown(body),\n         \"error\": error},\n        status_code=status_code,\n    )\n\n\nSTATUS_FILTERS = {\"all\": None, \"published\": posts.PUBLISHED, \"draft\": posts.DRAFT}\nTYPE_FILTERS = (\"all\", \"post\", \"page\")\n\n\n@router.get(\"\")\ndef post_list(request: Request, status: str = \"all\", kind: str = Query(\"all\", alias=\"type\"),\n              user=Depends(auth.require_user), conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    \"\"\"Content list. Unknown filter values fall back to All.\"\"\"\n    status = status if status in STATUS_FILTERS else \"all\"\n    kind = kind if kind in TYPE_FILTERS else \"all\"\n    own_only = None if user.role == \"admin\" else user.id\n    # Pages arrive in T05; until then the Page filter shows an empty list.\n    shows_posts = kind != \"page\"\n    counts = posts.count_by_status(conn, own_only) if shows_posts else {}\n    shown = posts.list_posts(conn, own_only, STATUS_FILTERS[status]) if shows_posts else []\n    published, drafts = counts.get(posts.PUBLISHED, 0), counts.get(posts.DRAFT, 0)\n    status_links = [(\"all\", \"All\", published + drafts), (\"published\", \"Published\", published),\n                    (\"draft\", \"Draft\", drafts)]\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/post_list.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Content list\", \"user\": user, \"posts\": shown, \"status\": status, \"type\": kind,\n         \"status_links\": status_links, \"type_links\": TYPE_FILTERS if user.role == \"admin\" else ()},\n    )\n\n\n@router.get(\"/new\")\ndef post_new(request: Request, user=Depends(auth.require_user)):\n    return _editor_page(request, user, None, \"\", \"\")\n\n\n@router.post(\"\")\ndef post_create(request: Request, title: str = Form(\"\"), body: str = Form(\"\"),\n                user=Depends(auth.require_user), conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    title = title.strip()\n    if not title:\n        return _editor_page(request, user, None, title, body, \"A Post needs a title\", 400)\n    post_id = posts.create_post(conn, title, body, user.id, auth.now(request))\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/preview\")\ndef post_preview(request: Request, title: str = Form(\"\"), body: str = Form(\"\"),\n                 post_id: int | None = Form(None),\n                 user=Depends(auth.require_user), conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    post = _own_post(conn, post_id, user) if post_id else None\n    return _editor_page(request, user, post, title, body)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{post_id}\")\ndef post_edit(post_id: int, request: Request, user=Depends(auth.require_user),\n              conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    post = _own_post(conn, post_id, user)\n    return _editor_page(request, user, post, post.title, post.body)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{post_id}\")\ndef post_update(post_id: int, request: Request, title: str = Form(\"\"), body: str = Form(\"\"),\n                slug: str | None = Form(None), user=Depends(auth.require_user), conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    post = _own_post(conn, post_id, user)\n    title = title.strip()\n    if not title:\n        return _editor_page(request, user, post, title, body, \"A Post needs a title\", 400)\n    new_slug = None\n    if user.role == \"admin\" and slug is not None and slug.strip():\n        new_slug = posts.slugify(slug)\n        if Passwords are hashed by users.create_user\n    and are never printed or stored as text.\n    \"\"\"\n    db.init_db(database)\n    conn = db.connect(database)\n    created = []\n    for role, (email, password) in accounts.items():\n        if not users.email_exists(conn, email):\n            users.create_user(conn, email, password, role)\n            created.append(email)\n    conn.close()\n    return created\n\"\"\"The FastAPI application.\n\nThe admin console answers at /admin and the public site answers at /. Keep this file small.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport time\nfrom contextlib import asynccontextmanager\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Callable\n\nfrom fastapi import Depends, FastAPI, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\nfrom starlette.middleware.sessions import SessionMiddleware\n\nfrom app import auth, db, settings\nfrom app.routes import admin as admin_routes\nfrom app.routes import auth as auth_routes\nfrom app.routes import posts as post_routes\nfrom app.templating import templates\n\n\ndef create_app(database: Path | None = None,\n               clock: Callable[[], float] | None = None) -> FastAPI:\n    settings.require_safe_secret_key()\n    database = Path(database or settings.DATABASE_PATH)\n\n    @asynccontextmanager\n    async def lifespan(_: FastAPI):\n        db.init_db(database)\n        yield\n\n    app = FastAPI(title=\"IPHS 400 MP2 CMS\", lifespan=lifespan,\n                  dependencies=[Depends(auth.csrf_protect)])\n    app.state.database = database\n    app.state.clock = clock or time.time\n    app.add_middleware(SessionMiddleware, secret_key=settings.SECRET_KEY,\n                       session_cookie=\"cms_session\", same_site=\"lax\",\n                       max_age=None)\n\n    @app.exception_handler(auth.NotSignedIn)\n    async def to_login(request: Request, exc: auth.NotSignedIn):\n        return RedirectResponse(\"/login\", status_code=303)\n\n    app.include_router(auth_routes.router)\n    app.include_router(admin_routes.router)\n    app.include_router(post_routes.router)\n\n    @app.get(\"/\")\n    def public_home(request: Request):\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\n            request, \"public/home.html\",\n            {\"title\": settings.SITE_TITLE, \"items\": []},\n        )\n\n    # Every admin route goes through here.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport secrets\n\nfrom fastapi import Depends, HTTPException, Request\nfrom jinja2 import pass_context\n\nfrom app import db, settings, users\n\nSAFE_METHODS = {\"GET\", \"HEAD\", \"OPTIONS\"}\n\n\nclass NotSignedIn(Exception):\n    \"\"\"Raised by the guard; the app turns it into a redirect to /login.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef ensure_csrf(request: Request) -> str:\n    token = request.session.get(\"csrf\")\n    if not token:\n        token = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)\n        request.session[\"csrf\"] = token\n    return token\n\n\n@pass_context\ndef csrf_token(context) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Use as {{ csrf_token() }} inside every state-changing form.\"\"\"\n    return ensure_csrf(context[\"request\"])\n\n\nasync def csrf_protect(request: Request) -> None:\n    \"\"\"App-wide: any request that is not GET/HEAD/OPTIONS needs a valid token.\"\"\"\n    if request.method in SAFE_METHODS:\n        return\n    if request.url.path.startswith(\"/admin\") and \"user_id\" not in request.session:\n        raise NotSignedIn  # anonymous: send to login rather than a CSRF error\n    form = await request.form()\n    sent = str(form.get(\"csrf_token\", \"\"))\n    expected = request.session.get(\"csrf\", \"\")\n    if not expected or not secrets.compare_digest(sent, expected):\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=\"Invalid or missing CSRF token\")\n\n\ndef now(request: Request) -> float:\n    return request.app.state.clock()\n\n\ndef start_session(request: Request, user: users.User) -> None:\n    request.session.clear()  # fresh session on sign-in\n    request.session[\"user_id\"] = user.id\n    request.session[\"last_seen\"] = now(request)\n    ensure_csrf(request)\n\n\ndef end_session(request: Request) -> None:\n    request.session.clear()\n\n\ndef require_user(request: Request, conn=Depends(db.get_db)) -> users.User:\n    \"\"\"Signed in, not idle too long, and still active; otherwise to the login page.\"\"\"\n    user_id = request.session.get(\"user_id\")\n    last_seen = request.session.get(\"last_seen\")\n    current = now(request)\n    if user_id is None or last_seen is None or current - last_seen > settings.IDLE_TIMEOUT_SECONDS:\n        end_session(request)\n        raise NotSignedIn\n    user = users.get_user(conn, user_id)\n    if user is None or not user.active:\n        end_session(request)\n        raise NotSignedIn\n    request.session[\"last_seen\"] = current\n    return user\n\n\ndef require_role(*roles: str):\n    \"\"\"Guard for role-restricted routes: Depends(require_role(\"admin\")).\"\"\"\n\n    def guard(user: users.User = Depends(require_user)) -> users.User:\n        if user.role not in roles:\n            raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=\"You do not have access to this page\")\n        return user\n\n    return guard\n\"\"\"Shared test fixtures.\n\n`client` gives you the app. `client_as(role)` gives you a client that is logged\nin as a seeded user of that role — it works as soon as your login route exists,\nso access-control tests stay one line:\n\n    def test_editor_cannot_manage_users(client_as):\n        assert client_as(\"editor\").get(\"/admin/users\").status_code in (302, 403)\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\n\nimport pytest\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n\nfrom app.main import create_app\n\n# Matches scripts/seed_demo.py. Passwords come from the environment there; in\n# tests they are fixed and meaningless.\nDEMO_USERS = {\n    \"admin\": {\"email\": \"admin@example.test\", \"password\": \"test-admin-pw\"},\n    \"editor\": {\"email\": \"editor@example.test\", \"password\": \"test-editor-pw\"},\n}\n\n\nclass FakeClock:\n    \"\"\"Injected clock so idle sign-out is tested without waiting.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, now: float = 1_000_000.0):\n        self.now = now\n\n    def __call__(self) -> float:\n        return self.now\n\n    def advance(self, seconds: float) -> None:\n        self.now += seconds\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef clock() -> FakeClock:\n    return FakeClock()\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef make_app(tmp_path, clock):\n    \"\"\"Factory for a fresh app on its own seeded database and the fake clock.\"\"\"\n    from app.seed import seed_users\n\n    def _make():\n        db = tmp_path / \"test.db\"\n        application = create_app(database=db, clock=clock)\n        seed_users(db, {role: (u[\"email\"], u[\"password\"])\n                        for role, u in DEMO_USERS.items()})\n        return application\n\n    return _make\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef client(make_app) -> TestClient:\n    return TestClient(make_app())\n\n\ndef csrf_of(client: TestClient, path: str = \"/login\") -> str:\n    \"\"\"Read the CSRF token out of the form on `path`.\"\"\"\n    html = client.get(path).text\n    return re.search(r'name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"([^\"]+)\"', html).group(1)\n\n\ndef sign_in(client: TestClient, email: str, password: str, token: str | None = None):\n One short-lived connection per request.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Iterator\n\nfrom fastapi import Request\n\nSCHEMA = \"\"\"\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (\n    id            INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n    email         TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,\n    password_hash TEXT NOT NULL,\n    role          TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (role IN ('admin', 'editor')),\n    active        INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1\n);\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS posts (\n    id         INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n    title      TEXT NOT NULL,\n    slug       TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,\n    body       TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',\n    status     TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft' CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'published')),\n    author_id  INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES users (id),\n    created_at REAL NOT NULL,\n    updated_at REAL NOT NULL\n);\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef connect(path: Path) -> sqlite3.Connection:\n    conn = sqlite3.connect(path, check_same_thread=False)\n    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n    return conn\n\n\ndef init_db(path: Path) -> None:\n    conn = connect(path)\n    with conn:\n        conn.executescript(SCHEMA)\n    conn.close()\n\n\ndef get_db(request: Request) -> Iterator[sqlite3.Connection]:\n    \"\"\"FastAPI dependency: a connection for this request, closed afterwards.\"\"\"\n    conn = connect(request.app.state.database)\n    try:\n        yield conn\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\"\"\"Posts: Markdown body, Draft/Published status, author, immutable slug.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\nimport sqlite3\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n\nDRAFT = \"draft\"\nPUBLISHED = \"published\"\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass Post:\n    id: int\n    title: str\n    slug: str\n    body: str\n    status: str\n    author_id: int\n    author_email: str\n    created_at: float\n    updated_at: float\n\n\n_SELECT = \"\"\"\nSELECT p.*, u.email AS author_email\nFROM posts p JOIN users u ON u.id = p.author_id\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef _row_to_post(row: sqlite3.Row) -> Post:\n    return Post(row[\"id\"], row[\"title\"], row[\"slug\"], row[\"body\"], row[\"status\"],\n                row[\"author_id\"], row[\"author_email\"], row[\"created_at\"], row[\"updated_at\"])\n\n\ndef slugify(title: str) -> str:\n    return re.sub(r\"[^a-z0-9]+\", \"-\", title.lower()).strip(\"-\") or \"post\"\n\n\ndef _unique_slug(conn: sqlite3.Connection, title: str) -> str:\n    base = slugify(title)\n    slug, n = base, 1\n    while conn.execute(\"SELECT 1 FROM posts WHERE slug = ?\", (slug,)).fetchone():\n        n += 1\n        slug = f\"{base}-{n}\"\n    return slug\n\n\ndef create_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, title: str, body: str, author_id: int,\n                now: float) -> int:\n    \"\"\"New Posts are always Drafts.\"\"\"\n    with conn:\n        cur = conn.execute(\n            \"INSERT INTO posts (title, slug, body, status, author_id, created_at, updated_at)\"\n            \" VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n            (title, _unique_slug(conn, title), body, DRAFT, author_id, now, now),\n        )\n    return cur.lastrowid\n\n\ndef get_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post_id: int) -> Post | None:\n    row = conn.execute(_SELECT + \" WHERE p.id = ?\", (post_id,)).fetchone()\n    return _row_to_post(row) if row else None\n\n\ndef list_posts(conn: sqlite3.Connection, author_id: int | None = None,\n               status: str | None = None) -> list[Post]:\n    \"\"\"Newest first. author_id limits the list to one User's Posts, status to one status.\"\"\"\n    clauses, args = [], []\n    if author_id is not None:\n        clauses.append(\"p.author_id = ?\")\n        args.append(author_id)\n    if status is not None:\n        clauses.append(\"p.status = ?\")\n        args.append(status)\n    where = \" WHERE \" + \" AND \".join(clauses) if clauses else \"\"\n    rows = conn.execute(_SELECT + where + \" ORDER BY p.created_at DESC, p.id DESC\",\n                        args).fetchall()\n    return [_row_to_post(r) for r in rows]\n\n\ndef count_by_status(conn: sqlite3.Connection, author_id: int | None = None) -> dict[str, int]:\n    \"\"\"Posts per status (draft, published), optionally for one author.\"\"\"\n    sql, args = \"SELECT status, COUNT(*) AS n FROM posts\", ()\n    if author_id is not None:\n        sql, args = sql + \" WHERE author_id = ?\", (author_id,)\n    found = {r[\"status\"]: r[\"n\"] for r in conn.execute(sql + \" GROUP BY status\", args)}\n    return {DRAFT: found.get(DRAFT, 0), PUBLISHED: found.get(PUBLISHED, 0)}\n\n\ndef update_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post_id: int, title: str, body: str,\n                now: float, status: str, slug: str | None = None) -> None:\n    \"\"\"The slug changes only when a normalised `slug` is passed (Admin-only callers).\"\"\"\n    with conn:\n        conn.execute(\n            \"UPDATE posts SET title = ?, body = ?, status = ?, updated_at = ?,\"\n            \" slug = COALESCE(?, slug) WHERE id = ?\",\n            (title, body, status, now, slug, post_id),\n        )\n\n\ndef set_status(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post_id: int, status: str, now: float) -> None:\n    with conn:\n        conn.execute(\"UPDATE posts SET status = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?\",\n                     (status, now, post_id))\n\n\ndef slug_taken(conn: sqlite3.Connection, slug: str, post_id: int) -> bool:\n    return conn.execute(\"SELECT 1 FROM posts WHERE slug = ? AND id != ?\",\n                        (slug, post_id)).fetchone() is not None\n\n\ndef delete_post(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post_id: int) -> None:\n    with conn:\n        conn.execute(\"DELETE FROM posts WHERE id = ?\", (post_id,))\n\"\"\"Console routes for Posts: list, create, edit, preview, delete.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Form, HTTPException, Query, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\n\nfrom app import auth, db, posts\nfrom app.markdown import render_markdown\nfrom app.templating import templates\n\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/admin/posts\")\n\nNOT_YOURS = \"You can only open your own Posts\"\n\n\ndef _own_post(conn, post_id: int, user) -> posts.Post:\n    \"\"\"The Post, or a refusal. Editors reach only their own; Admins reach all.\"\"\"\n    post = posts.get_post(conn, post_id)\n    if post is None:\n        # An Editor gets the same refusal as for someone else's Post, so ids do not leak.\n        if user.role != \"admin\":\n            raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=NOT_YOURS)\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=\"No such Post\")\n    if user.role != \"admin\" and post.author_id != user.id:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=NOT_YOURS)\n    return post\n\n\ndef _editor_page(request, user, post, title, body, error=None, status_code=200):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/post_edit.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Edit Post\" if post else \"New Post\", \"user\": user, \"post\": post,\n         \"form_title\": title, \"form_body\": body, \"preview_html\": render_markdown(body),\n         \"error\": error},\n        status_code=status_code,\n    )\n\n\nSTATUS_FILTERS = {\"all\": None, \"published\": posts.PUBLISHED, \"draft\": posts.DRAFT}\nTYPE_FILTERS = (\"all\", \"post\", \"page\")\n\n\n@router.get(\"\")\ndef post_list(request: Request, status: str = \"all\", kind: str = Query(\"all\", alias=\"type\"),\n              user=Depends(auth.require_user), conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    \"\"\"Content list. posts.slug_taken(conn, new_slug, post_id):\n            return _editor_page(request, user, post, title, body,\n                                f\"The slug “{new_slug}” is already used by another Post\", 400)\n    # An Editor's edit sends a Published Post back to Draft; an Admin's leaves it alone.\n    status = post.status if user.role == \"admin\" else posts.DRAFT\n    posts.update_post(conn, post_id, title, body, auth.now(request), status, new_slug)\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\ndef _deletable(post: posts.Post, user) -> posts.Post:\n    if user.role != \"admin\" and post.status != posts.DRAFT:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=\"Only a President can delete a Published Post\")\n    return post\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{post_id}/delete\")\ndef post_delete_confirm(post_id: int, request: Request, user=Depends(auth.require_user),\n                        conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    post = _deletable(_own_post(conn, post_id, user), user)\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/post_delete.html\", {\"title\": \"Delete Post\", \"user\": user, \"post\": post}\n    )\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{post_id}/delete\")\ndef post_delete(post_id: int, user=Depends(auth.require_user), conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    post = _deletable(_own_post(conn, post_id, user), user)\n    posts.delete_post(conn, post.id)\n    return RedirectResponse(\"/admin/posts\", status_code=303)\n\n\nCONFIRMS = {\n    \"publish\": (\"Publish this?\", \"Yes, publish it\", posts.DRAFT, posts.PUBLISHED,\n                \"It becomes Live at the next Publish run.\"),\n    \"unpublish\": (\"Are you sure you want to unpublish this?\", \"Yes, unpublish it\",\n                  posts.PUBLISHED, posts.DRAFT,\n                  \"It returns to Draft and leaves the public site at the next Publish run.\"),\n}\n\nadmin_only = Depends(auth.require_role(\"admin\"))\n\n\ndef _confirm_page(action: str, post_id: int, request, user, conn):\n    heading, button, _, _, note = CONFIRMS[action]\n    post = _own_post(conn, post_id, user)\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/post_confirm.html\",\n        {\"title\": heading, \"user\": user, \"post\": post, \"action\": action,\n         \"heading\": heading, \"button\": button, \"note\": note},\n    )\n\n\ndef _change_status(action: str, post_id: int, request, user, conn):\n    _, _, from_status, to_status, _ = CONFIRMS[action]\n    post = _own_post(conn, post_id, user)\n    if post.status == from_status:\n        posts.set_status(conn, post_id, to_status, auth.now(request))\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{post_id}/publish\")\ndef post_publish_confirm(post_id: int, request: Request, user=admin_only,\n                         conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    return _confirm_page(\"publish\", post_id, request, user, conn)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{post_id}/publish\")\ndef post_publish(post_id: int, request: Request, user=admin_only, conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    return _change_status(\"publish\", post_id, request, user, conn)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{post_id}/unpublish\")\ndef post_unpublish_confirm(post_id: int, request: Request, user=admin_only,\n                           conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    return _confirm_page(\"unpublish\", post_id, request, user, conn)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{post_id}/unpublish\")\ndef post_unpublish(post_id: int, request: Request, user=admin_only, conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    return _change_status(\"unpublish\", post_id, request, user, conn)\n\"\"\"The admin console's home page and the /admin catch-all.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Request\n\nfrom app import auth, db, posts, users\nfrom app.templating import templates\n\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/admin\")\n\n# Include this router LAST: an unknown /admin address still sends anonymous\n# visitors to the login page instead of a bare 404.\ncatch_all = APIRouter(prefix=\"/admin\")\n\n\n@router.get(\"\")\ndef admin_home(request: Request, user=Depends(auth.require_user), conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    is_admin = user.role == \"admin\"\n    counts = posts.count_by_status(conn, None if is_admin else user.id)\n    active, deactivated = users.count_by_active(conn) if is_admin else (None, None)\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/hello.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Admin\", \"user\": user, \"drafts\": counts[posts.DRAFT],\n         \"published\": counts[posts.PUBLISHED], \"active_users\": active,\n         \"deactivated_users\": deactivated},\n    )\n\n\n@catch_all.api_route(\"/{rest:path}\", include_in_schema=False,\n                     methods=[\"GET\", \"POST\", \"PUT\", \"PATCH\", \"DELETE\"],\n                     dependencies=[Depends(auth.require_user)])\ndef admin_unknown(rest: str):\n    raise HTTPException(status_code=404)\n\"\"\"Create the demo President and Officer. Safe to run repeatedly.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom app import db, users\n\n\ndef seed_users(database: Path, accounts: dict[str, tuple[str, str]]) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"accounts maps role -> (email, password). Existing emails are left alone.\n\n    Returns the emails that were created. Passwords are hashed by users.create_user\n    and are never printed or stored as text.\n    \"\"\"\n    db.init_db(database)\n    conn = db.connect(database)\n    created = []\n    for role, (email, password) in accounts.items():\n        if not users.email_exists(conn, email):\n            users.create_user(conn, email, password, role)\n            created.append(email)\n    conn.close()\n    return created\n\"\"\"The FastAPI application.\n\nThe admin console answers at /admin and the public site answers at /. Sign-in,\nsessions, CSRF and the permission guard live in app/auth.py; add routes in their\nown modules (app/routes/...) and include them here. Keep this file small.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport time\nfrom contextlib import asynccontextmanager\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Callable\n\nfrom fastapi import Depends, FastAPI, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\nfrom starlette.middleware.sessions import SessionMiddleware\n\nfrom app import auth, db, settings\nfrom app.routes import admin as admin_routes\nfrom app.routes import auth as auth_routes\nfrom app.routes import posts as post_routes\nfrom app.templating import templates\n\n\ndef create_app(database: Path | None = None,\n               clock: Callable[[], float] | None = None) -> FastAPI:\n    settings.require_safe_secret_key()\n    database = Path(database or settings.DATABASE_PATH)\n\n    @asynccontextmanager\n    async def lifespan(_: FastAPI):\n        db.init_db(database)\n        yield\n\n    app = FastAPI(title=\"IPHS 400 MP2 CMS\", lifespan=lifespan,\n                  dependencies=[Depends(auth.csrf_protect)])\n    app.state.database = database\n    app.state.clock = clock or time.time\n    app.add_middleware(SessionMiddleware, secret_key=settings.SECRET_KEY,\n                       session_cookie=\"cms_session\", same_site=\"lax\",\n                       max_age=None)\n\n    @app.exception_handler(auth.NotSignedIn)\n    async def to_login(request: Request, exc: auth.NotSignedIn):\n        return RedirectResponse(\"/login\", status_code=303)\n\n    app.include_router(auth_routes.router)\n    app.include_router(admin_routes.router)\n    app.include_router(post_routes.router)\n\n    @app.get(\"/\")\n    def public_home(request: Request):\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\n            request, \"public/home.html\",\n            {\"title\": settings.SITE_TITLE, \"items\": []},\n        )\n\n    # Later ticket routers plug in here, above the catch-all.\n    app.include_router(admin_routes.catch_all)  # keep LAST\n\n    return app\n\n\napp = create_app()\n\"\"\"Sessions, CSRF and the permission guard. Every admin route goes through here.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport secrets\n\nfrom fastapi import Depends, HTTPException, Request\nfrom jinja2 import pass_context\n\nfrom app import db, settings, users\n\nSAFE_METHODS = {\"GET\", \"HEAD\", \"OPTIONS\"}\n\n\nclass NotSignedIn(Exception):\n    \"\"\"Raised by the guard; the app turns it into a redirect to /login.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef ensure_csrf(request: Request) -> str:\n    token = request.session.get(\"csrf\")\n    if not token:\n        token = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)\n        request.session[\"csrf\"] = token\n    return token\n\n\n@pass_context\ndef csrf_token(context) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Use as {{ csrf_token() }} inside every state-changing form.\"\"\"\n    return ensure_csrf(context[\"request\"])\n\n\nasync def csrf_protect(request: Request) -> None:\n    \"\"\"App-wide: any request that is not GET/HEAD/OPTIONS needs a valid token.\"\"\"\n    if request.method in SAFE_METHODS:\n        return\n    if request.url.path.startswith(\"/admin\") and \"user_id\" not in request.session:\n        raise NotSignedIn  # anonymous: send to login rather than a CSRF error\n    form = await request.form()\n    sent = str(form.get(\"csrf_token\", \"\"))\n    expected = request.session.get(\"csrf\", \"\")\n    if not expected or not secrets.compare_digest(sent, expected):\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=\"Invalid or missing CSRF token\")\n\n\ndef now(request: Request) -> float:\n    return request.app.state.clock()\n\n\ndef start_session(request: Request, user: users.User) -> None:\n    request.session.clear()  # fresh session on sign-in\n    request.session[\"user_id\"] = user.id\n    request.session[\"last_seen\"] = now(request)\n    ensure_csrf(request)\n\n\ndef end_session(request: Request) -> None:\n    request.session.clear()\n\n\ndef require_user(request: Request, conn=Depends(db.get_db)) -> users.User:\n    \"\"\"Signed in, not idle too long, and still active; otherwise to the login page.\"\"\"\n    user_id = request.session.get(\"user_id\")\n    last_seen = request.session.get(\"last_seen\")\n    current = now(request)\n    if user_id is None or last_seen is None or current - last_seen > settings.IDLE_TIMEOUT_SECONDS:\n        end_session(request)\n        raise NotSignedIn\n    user = users.get_user(conn, user_id)\n    if user is None or not user.active:\n        end_session(request)\n        raise NotSignedIn\n    request.session[\"last_seen\"] = current\n    return user\n\n\ndef require_role(*roles: str):\n    \"\"\"Guard for role-restricted routes: Depends(require_role(\"admin\")).\"\"\"\n\n    def guard(user: users.User = Depends(require_user)) -> users.User:\n        if user.role not in roles:\n            raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=\"You do not have access to this page\")\n        return user\n\n    return guard\n\"\"\"Shared test fixtures.\n\n`client` gives you the app. `client_as(role)` gives you a client that is logged\nin as a seeded user of that role — it works as soon as your login route exists,\nso access-control tests stay one line:\n\n    def test_editor_cannot_manage_users(client_as):\n        assert client_as(\"editor\").get(\"/admin/users\").status_code in (302, 403)\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\n\nimport pytest\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n\nfrom app.main import create_app\n\n# Matches scripts/seed_demo.py. Passwords come from the environment there; in\n# tests they are fixed and meaningless.\nDEMO_USERS = {\n    \"admin\": {\"email\": \"admin@example.test\", \"password\": \"test-admin-pw\"},\n    \"editor\": {\"email\": \"editor@example.test\", \"password\": \"test-editor-pw\"},\n}\n\n\nclass FakeClock:\n    \"\"\"Injected clock so idle sign-out is tested without waiting.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, now: float = 1_000_000.0):\n        self.now = now\n\n    def __call__(self) -> float:\n        return self.now\n\n    def advance(self, seconds: float) -> None:\n        self.now += seconds\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef clock() -> FakeClock:\n    return FakeClock()\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef make_app(tmp_path, clock):\n    \"\"\"Factory for a fresh app on its own seeded database and the fake clock.\"\"\"\n    from app.seed import seed_users\n\n    def _make():\n        db = tmp_path / \"test.db\"\n        application = create_app(database=db, clock=clock)\n        seed_users(db, {role: (u[\"email\"], u[\"password\"])\n                        for role, u in DEMO_USERS.items()})\n        return application\n\n    return _make\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef client(make_app) -> TestClient:\n    return TestClient(make_app())\n\n\ndef csrf_of(client: TestClient, path: str = \"/login\") -> str:\n    \"\"\"Read the CSRF token out of the form on `path`.\"\"\"\n    html = client.get(path).text\n    return re.search(r'name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"([^\"]+)\"', html).group(1)\n\n\ndef sign_in(client: TestClient, email: str, password: str, token: str | None = None):\n    return client.post(\n        \"/login\",\n        data={\"email\": email, \"password\": password,\n              \"csrf_token\": token or csrf_of(client)},\n        follow_redirects=False,\n    )\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef client_as(make_app):\n    \"\"\"Return a factory: client_as(\"editor\") -> a logged-in TestClient.\"\"\"\n\n    def _login(role: str) -> TestClient:\n        user = DEMO_USERS[role]\n        c = TestClient(make_app())\n        response = sign_in(c, user[\"email\"], user[\"password\"])\n        assert response.status_code in (200, 302, 303), (\n            f\"Login as {role} failed with {response.status_code}\")\n        return c\n\n    return html>\n<html lang=\"en\">\n<head>\n  <meta charset=\"utf-8\">\n  <meta name=\"viewport\" content=\"width=device-width, initial-scale=1\">\n  <title>{% block title %}{{ title }}{% endblock %}</title>\n  <link rel=\"stylesheet\" href=\"{{ css_path | default('style.css') }}\">\n</head>\n<body>\n  <header><a href=\"{{ home_path | default('index.html') }}\">{{ title }}</a></header>\n  <main>{% block content %}{% endblock %}</main>\n  <footer><small>IPHS 400 Mini-Project #2</small></footer>\n</body>\n</html>\n{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>Content list</h1>\n  <p><a href=\"/admin/posts/new\">New Post</a> · <a href=\"/admin\">Dashboard</a></p>\n  <nav aria-label=\"Status\">\n    {% for key, label, n in status_links %}\n    <a href=\"/admin/posts?status={{ key }}&amp;type={{ type }}\">{{ label }} ({{ n }})</a>{% if not loop.last %} · {% endif %}\n    {% endfor %}\n  </nav>\n  <nav aria-label=\"Type\">\n    {% for key in type_links %}\n    <a href=\"/admin/posts?status={{ status }}&amp;type={{ key }}\">{{ key | capitalize }}</a>{% if not loop.last %} · {% endif %}\n    {% endfor %}\n  </nav>\n  {% if posts %}\n  <ul>\n    {% for post in posts %}\n    <li>\n      <a href=\"/admin/posts/{{ post.id }}\">{{ post.title }}</a>\n      — {{ post.status | capitalize }}, by {{ post.author_email }}\n    </li>\n    {% endfor %}\n  </ul>\n  {% else %}\n  <p>Nothing here yet.</p>\n  {% endif %}\n{% endblock %}\n{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>{{ title }}</h1>\n  <p><a href=\"/admin/posts\">All Posts</a></p>\n  {% if error %}<p role=\"alert\">{{ error }}</p>{% endif %}\n  <div style=\"display: flex; gap: 1rem; flex-wrap: wrap;\">\n    <form method=\"post\" action=\"{{ '/admin/posts/' ~ post.id if post else '/admin/posts' }}\"\n          style=\"flex: 1 1 20rem;\">\n      <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token() }}\">\n      {% if post %}<input type=\"hidden\" name=\"post_id\" value=\"{{ post.id }}\">{% endif %}\n      <p><label>Title<br>\n        <input type=\"text\" name=\"title\" value=\"{{ form_title }}\" required style=\"width: 100%;\"></label></p>\n      {% if post %}\n      {% if user.role == 'admin' %}\n      <p><label>Slug<br>\n        <input type=\"text\" name=\"slug\" value=\"{{ post.slug }}\" style=\"width: 100%;\"></label></p>\n      {% else %}\n      <p>Slug: <code>{{ post.slug }}</code> (set when first saved)</p>\n      {% endif %}\n      <p>Status: {{ post.status | capitalize }}</p>\n      {% endif %}\n      <p><label>Body (Markdown)<br>\n        <textarea name=\"body\" rows=\"14\" style=\"width: 100%;\">{{ form_body }}</textarea></label></p>\n      <p>\n        <button type=\"submit\">Save Draft</button>\n        <button type=\"submit\" formaction=\"/admin/posts/preview\">Preview</button>\n      </p>\n    </form>\n    <section style=\"flex: 1 1 20rem;\" aria-label=\"Preview\">\n      <h2>Preview</h2>\n      {% if not post or post.status == 'draft' %}\n      <p><strong>Draft, not Live</strong></p>\n      {% else %}\n      <p><strong>Published</strong> (Live after the next Publish run)</p>\n      {% endif %}\n      {{ preview_html | safe }}\n    </section>\n  </div>\n  {% if post %}\n  <p>\n    {% if user.role == 'admin' %}\n      {% if post.status == 'draft' %}<a href=\"/admin/posts/{{ post.id }}/publish\">Publish</a> ·\n      {% else %}<a href=\"/admin/posts/{{ post.id }}/unpublish\">Unpublish</a> ·{% endif %}\n    {% endif %}\n    {% if user.role == 'admin' or post.status == 'draft' %}\n    <a href=\"/admin/posts/{{ post.id }}/delete\">Delete this Post</a>\n    {% endif %}\n  </p>\n  {% endif %}\n{% endblock %}\n{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>{{ heading }}</h1>\n  <p>“{{ post.title }}” — {{ note }}</p>\n  <form method=\"post\" action=\"/admin/posts/{{ post.id }}/{{ action }}\">\n    <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token() }}\">\n    <button type=\"submit\">{{ button }}</button>\n    <a href=\"/admin/posts/{{ post.id }}\">Cancel</a>\n  </form>\n{% endblock %}\n{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>Delete this Post?</h1>\n  <p>Are you sure you want to permanently delete “{{ post.title }}”? This cannot be undone.</p>\n  <form method=\"post\" action=\"/admin/posts/{{ post.id }}/delete\">\n    <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token() }}\">\n    <button type=\"submit\">Yes, delete it</button>\n    <a href=\"/admin/posts/{{ post.id }}\">Cancel</a>\n  </form>\n{% endblock %}\n\"\"\"T04: Dashboard counts and the filterable Content list. Seam 1: the HTTP app.\"\"\"\nimport re\n\nfrom app import db\nfrom tests.test_t02_posts import create_post, post_url\nfrom tests.test_t03_president_control import act, president_for\n\n\ndef count_of(html: str, label: str) -> int:\n    \"\"\"The number printed next to a label such as 'Drafts: 3'.\"\"\"\n    return int(re.search(rf\"{label}:\\s*(\\d+)\", html).group(1))\n\n\ndef titles_in_order(html: str, titles: list[str]) -> list[str]:\n    return sorted((t for t in titles if t in html), key=html.index)\n\n\ndef test_dashboard_counts_follow_post_changes(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    admin = president_for(editor)\n    assert count_of(admin.get(\"/admin\").text, \"Drafts\") == 0\n    url = post_url(create_post(editor, title=\"One\"))\n    create_post(editor, title=\"Two\")\n    page = admin.get(\"/admin\").text\n    assert (count_of(page, \"Drafts\"), count_of(page, \"Published\")) == (2, 0)\n    act(admin, url, \"publish\")\n    page = admin.get(\"/admin\").text\n    assert (count_of(page, \"Drafts\"), count_of(page, \"Published\")) == (1, 1)\n\n\ndef test_dashboard_has_a_new_post_button_that_opens_the_editor(client_as):\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    page = c.get(\"/admin\").text\n    assert 'href=\"/admin/posts/new\"' in page\n    assert c.get(\"/admin/posts/new\").status_code == 200\n\n\ndef test_president_sees_user_counts_officer_does_not(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    admin = president_for(editor)\n    page = admin.get(\"/admin\").text\n    assert (count_of(page, \"Active Users\"), count_of(page, \"Deactivated Users\")) == (2, 0)\n    conn = db.connect(editor.app.state.database)\n    with conn:\n        conn.execute(\"UPDATE users SET active = 0 WHERE role = 'editor'\")\n    conn.close()\n    page = admin.get(\"/admin\").text\n    assert (count_of(page, \"Active Users\"), count_of(page, \"Deactivated Users\")) == (1, 1)\n    assert \"Active Users\" not in editor.get(\"/admin\").text\n    assert \"Deactivated Users\" not in editor.get(\"/admin\").text\n\n\ndef test_content_list_is_newest_first_with_status_links_and_counts(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    admin = president_for(editor)\n    old = post_url(create_post(editor, title=\"Older post\"))\n    create_post(editor, title=\"Newer post\")\n    act(admin, old, \"publish\")\n    page = admin.get(\"/admin/posts\").text\n    assert titles_in_order(page, [\"Older post\", \"Newer post\"]) == [\"Newer post\", \"Older
