{"type":"api-request-blob","sessionId":"2ccec162-c0eb-4cb8-a952-47bc94d99a51","hash":"c5e2ddc583e33f147e32c030473207f0cc1111a7ffeb992d2334e0953736bea7","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01KN3eX9poyV9u4pWHg8e9bs","type":"tool_result","content":"\"\"\"T03: Presidents publish, unpublish and delete Posts. Seam 1: the HTTP app.\"\"\"\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n\nfrom tests.conftest import csrf_of, sign_in\nfrom tests.test_t02_posts import create_post, post_url\n\n\ndef president_for(editor) -> TestClient:\n    \"\"\"A President on the same app and database as `editor`.\"\"\"\n    admin = TestClient(editor.app)\n    sign_in(admin, \"admin@example.test\", \"test-admin-pw\")\n    return admin\n\n\ndef act(c, url, action, **extra):\n    token = csrf_of(c, url)\n    return c.post(f\"{url}/{action}\", data={\"csrf_token\": token, **extra},\n                  follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef published_post(editor, title=\"Pitch: NVDA\"):\n    url = post_url(create_post(editor, title=title))\n    admin = president_for(editor)\n    assert act(admin, url, \"publish\").status_code == 303\n    assert \"Published\" in admin.get(url).text\n    return url, admin\n\n\ndef test_president_publishes_a_draft_after_a_confirm(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    url = post_url(create_post(editor))\n    admin = president_for(editor)\n    confirm = admin.get(url + \"/publish\")\n    assert confirm.status_code == 200\n    assert \"Publish this?\" in confirm.text\n    assert 'name=\"csrf_token\"' in confirm.text\n    assert \"Status: Draft\" in admin.get(url).text  # a GET publishes nothing\n    assert act(admin, url, \"publish\").status_code == 303\n    assert \"Status: Published\" in admin.get(url).text\n\n\ndef test_officer_cannot_publish_even_by_direct_post(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    url = post_url(create_post(editor))\n    assert editor.get(url + \"/publish\").status_code == 403\n    assert act(editor, url, \"publish\").status_code == 403\n    assert \"Status: Draft\" in editor.get(url).text\n\n\ndef test_president_unpublishes_a_published_post(client_as):\n\"\"\"T02: Officers write and edit their own Post drafts. Seam 1: the HTTP app.\"\"\"\nimport re\n\nfrom tests.conftest import csrf_of\n\n\ndef create_post(c, title=\"Meeting announcement\", body=\"Come to **Oden 110**.\"):\n    token = csrf_of(c, \"/admin/posts/new\")\n    return c.post(\"/admin/posts\", data={\"title\": title, \"body\": body, \"csrf_token\": token},\n                  follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef post_url(response) -> str:\n    assert response.status_code == 303\n    return response.headers[\"location\"]\n\n\ndef test_officer_creates_a_draft_they_own(client_as):\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    url = post_url(create_post(c))\n    assert re.fullmatch(r\"/admin/posts/\\d+\", url)\n    page = c.get(url)\n    assert page.status_code == 200\n    assert \"Meeting announcement\" in page.text\n    assert \"Draft\" in page.text\n    listing = c.get(\"/admin/posts\").text\n    assert \"Meeting announcement\" in listing\n    assert \"editor@example.test\" in listing\n\n\ndef test_slug_is_made_once_unique_and_survives_title_edits(client_as):\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    first = post_url(create_post(c, title=\"Spring Stock Pitch!\"))\n    second = post_url(create_post(c, title=\"Spring Stock Pitch!\"))\n    assert \"spring-stock-pitch\" in c.get(first).text\n    assert \"spring-stock-pitch-2\" in c.get(second).text\n    token = csrf_of(c, first)\n    c.post(first, data={\"title\": \"Totally different\", \"body\": \"x\", \"csrf_token\": token})\n    edited = c.get(first).text\n    assert \"Totally different\" in edited\n#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Create demo data so a grader (and you) can use the CMS immediately.\n\n    uv run python scripts/seed_demo.py\n\nT01 creates the users. Later tickets extend this so it also creates:\n  - (done in T01) one admin and one editor, passwords read from the environment\n  - a few posts and pages, at least one draft and one published\n\nThe rubric expects this to run clean on a fresh clone with .env.example values\n(item E4), because the database itself is never committed.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport os\nimport sys\n\n\ndef main() -> int:\n    admin_pw = os.environ.get(\"CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD\")\n    editor_pw = os.environ.get(\"CMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD\")\n    if not admin_pw or not editor_pw:\n        print(\"Set CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD and CMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD in .env \"\n              \"(copy .env.example).\")\n        return 1\n\n    # Local import so the script runs from a plain `uv run python scripts/...`.\n    from app import settings\n    from app.seed import seed_users\n\n    created = seed_users(settings.DATABASE_PATH, {\n        \"admin\": (os.environ.get(\"CMS_ADMIN_EMAIL\", \"admin@example.test\"), admin_pw),\n        \"editor\": (os.environ.get(\"CMS_EDITOR_EMAIL\", \"editor@example.test\"), editor_pw),\n    })\n    print(f\"Users created: {', '.join(created) or 'none (already present)'}\")\n    # TODO (later tickets): demo posts and pages, one draft and one published.\n    return 0\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    sys.exit(main())\n\"\"\"The `cms` command: serve, publish, deploy.\n\n    uv run cms serve      # admin console + public preview at http://localhost:8000\n    uv run cms publish    # render site/ from published content\n    uv run cms deploy     # push site/ to the gh-pages branch (Pages serves it)\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport argparse\nimport subprocess\nimport sys\n\nfrom app import settings\nfrom app.publish import render_site\n\n\ndef main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog=\"cms\")\n    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest=\"command\", required=True)\n    serve = sub.add_parser(\"serve\", help=\"run the admin console locally\")\n    serve.add_argument(\"--port\", type=int, default=8000)\n    serve.add_argument(\"--reload\", action=\"store_true\", default=True)\n    sub.add_parser(\"publish\", help=\"render site/ from published content\")\n    deploy = sub.add_parser(\"deploy\", help=\"push site/ to gh-pages\")\n    deploy.add_argument(\"--message\", default=\"Publish site\")\n    args = parser.parse_args(argv)\n\n    if args.command == \"serve\":\n        try:\n            settings.require_safe_secret_key()\n        except RuntimeError as problem:\n            print(problem)\n            return 1\n        import uvicorn\n\n        uvicorn.run(\"app.main:app\", port=args.port, reload=args.reload)\n        return 0\n\n    if args.command == \"publish\":\n        out = render_site()\n        print(f\"Wrote {out}. Preview it with:  python3 -m http.server -d {out} 8001\")\n        return 0\n\n    if args.command == \"deploy\":\n        if not settings.SITE.exists():\n            print(\"site/ does not exist yet — run `uv run cms publish` first.\")\n            return 1\n        result = subprocess.run(\n            [sys.executable, \"-m\", \"ghp_import\", \"-n\", \"-p\", \"-m\", args.message,\n             str(settings.SITE)],\n        )\n        if result.returncode == 0:\n            print(\"Pushed to gh-pages. Settings -> Pages -> Deploy from a branch -> \"\n                  \"gh-pages / root, then wait up to 10 minutes.\")\n        return result.returncode\n    return 1\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    raise SystemExit(main())\n\"\"\"Render the public site into site/ as plain HTML.\n\nT00 publishes a placeholder home page. As you build content types, extend\nrender_site() to write one file per published item. Two rules the rubric checks:\n\n  1. Only PUBLISHED content is written here. A draft that reaches site/ is a bug.\n  2. Every href and src is RELATIVE (\"style.css\", \"posts/x.html\"), never\n     root-absolute (\"/style.css\"), because Pages serves this from a subfolder.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport shutil\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom jinja2 import Environment, FileSystemLoader, select_autoescape\n\nfrom app import settings\n\nCSS = \"\"\"/* Minimal starter styles — make them yours. */\n:root { color-scheme: light dark; }\nbody { font: 16px/1.6 system-ui, sans-serif; margin: 0 auto; max-width: 42rem; padding: 1rem; }\nheader a { font-weight: 700; text-decoration: none; }\nmain { margin-block: 2rem; }\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef environment() -> Environment:\n    return Environment(\n        loader=FileSystemLoader(str(settings.TEMPLATES)),\n        autoescape=select_autoescape([\"html\"]),\n    )\n\n\ndef render_site(out: Path | None = None) -> Path:\n    out = out or settings.SITE\n    if out.exists():\n        shutil.rmtree(out)\n    out.mkdir(parents=True)\n    env = environment()\n    (out / \"style.css\").write_text(CSS)\n    (out / \"index.html\").write_text(\n        env.get_template(\"public/home.html\").render(\n            title=settings.SITE_TITLE, items=[], css_path=\"style.css\",\n            home_path=\"index.html\",\n        )\n    )\n    return out\n    assert titles_in_order(page, [\"Older post\", \"Newer post\"]) == [\"Newer post\", \"Older post\"]\n    for label, n in ((\"All\", 2), (\"Published\", 1), (\"Draft\", 1)):\n        assert re.search(rf'<a href=\"[^\"]*status=\\w+[^\"]*\">{label} \\({n}\\)</a>', page), label\n\n\ndef test_status_filter_limits_the_list(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    admin = president_for(editor)\n    live = post_url(create_post(editor, title=\"Live one\"))\n    create_post(editor, title=\"Draft one\")\n    act(admin, live, \"publish\")\n    published = admin.get(\"/admin/posts?status=published\").text\n    assert \"Live one\" in published and \"Draft one\" not in published\n    drafts = admin.get(\"/admin/posts?status=draft\").text\n    assert \"Draft one\" in drafts and \"Live one\" not in drafts\n    both = admin.get(\"/admin/posts?status=all\").text\n    assert \"Live one\" in both and \"Draft one\" in both\n    assert \"Live one\" in admin.get(\"/admin/posts?status=bogus\").text  # unknown means All\n\n\ndef test_type_filter_limits_to_posts_or_pages_without_breaking(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    admin = president_for(editor)\n    create_post(editor, title=\"A post\")\n    assert \"A post\" in admin.get(\"/admin/posts?type=post\").text\n    pages = admin.get(\"/admin/posts?type=page\")\n    assert pages.status_code == 200\n    assert \"A post\" not in pages.text\n    assert admin.get(\"/admin/posts?type=bogus\").status_code == 200\n\n\ndef test_officer_list_and_counts_include_only_their_own_posts(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    admin = president_for(editor)\n    create_post(editor, title=\"Mine\")\n    create_post(admin, title=\"President only\")\n    listing = editor.get(\"/admin/posts\").text\n    assert \"Mine\" in listing and \"President only\" not in listing\n    assert count_of(editor.get(\"/admin\").text, \"Drafts\") == 1\n    assert count_of(admin.get(\"/admin\").text, \"Drafts\") == 2\n    assert re.search(r\">All \\(1\\)</a>\", listing)\n    assert \"President only\" not in editor.get(\"/admin/posts?status=draft\").text\n\n\ndef test_officer_published_count_and_filters_cover_only_their_posts(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    admin = president_for(editor)\n    live = post_url(create_post(editor, title=\"Mine live\"))\n    create_post(editor, title=\"Mine draft\")\n    other = post_url(create_post(admin, title=\"Theirs live\"))\n    act(admin, live, \"publish\")\n    act(admin, other, \"publish\")\n    assert count_of(editor.get(\"/admin\").text, \"Published\") == 1\n    published = editor.get(\"/admin/posts?status=published\").text\n    assert \"Mine live\" in published and \"Theirs live\" not in published\n    assert \"Mine draft\" not in published\n    assert \"Theirs live\" not in editor.get(\"/admin/posts?type=post\").text\n    assert editor.get(\"/admin/posts?type=page\").status_code == 200\n    assert \"type=page\" not in editor.get(\"/admin/posts\").text  # Editors see Posts only\n{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>Hello admin</h1>\n  <h2>Dashboard</h2>\n  <p>Signed in as {{ user.email }} ({{ user.role }}).</p>\n  <p><a href=\"/admin/posts/new\">New Post</a> · <a href=\"/admin/posts\">Content list</a></p>\n  <ul>\n    <li>Drafts: {{ drafts }}</li>\n    <li>Published: {{ published }}</li>\n    {% if active_users is not none %}\n    <li>Active Users: {{ active_users }}</li>\n    <li>Deactivated Users: {{ deactivated_users }}</li>\n    {% endif %}\n  </ul>\n  <form method=\"post\" action=\"/logout\">\n    <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token() }}\">\n    <button type=\"submit\">Sign out</button>\n  </form>\n{% endblock %}","is_error":false}]}}

{"parentUuid":"3a6cfc11-54f6-4d95-b17c-67dd915f3826","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"c231bb73-8d53-4531-b686-e572c28d9639","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01KN3eX9poyV9u4pWHg8e9bs","type":"tool_result","content":"\"\"\"T03: Presidents publish, unpublish and delete Posts. Seam 1: the HTTP app.\"\"\"\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n\nfrom tests.conftest import csrf_of, sign_in\nfrom tests.test_t02_posts import create_post, post_url\n\n\ndef president_for(editor) -> TestClient:\n    \"\"\"A President on the same app and database as `editor`.\"\"\"\n    admin = TestClient(editor.app)\n    sign_in(admin, \"admin@example.test\", \"test-admin-pw\")\n    return admin\n\n\ndef act(c, url, action, **extra):\n    token = csrf_of(c, url)\n    return c.post(f\"{url}/{action}\", data={\"csrf_token\": token, **extra},\n                  follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef published_post(editor, title=\"Pitch: NVDA\"):\n    url = post_url(create_post(editor, title=title))\n    admin = president_for(editor)\n    assert act(admin, url, \"publish\").status_code == 303\n    assert \"Published\" in admin.get(url).text\n    return url, admin\n\n\ndef test_president_publishes_a_draft_after_a_confirm(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    url = post_url(create_post(editor))\n    admin = president_for(editor)\n    confirm = admin.get(url + \"/publish\")\n    assert confirm.status_code == 200\n    assert \"Publish this?\" in confirm.text\n    assert 'name=\"csrf_token\"' in confirm.text\n    assert \"Status: Draft\" in admin.get(url).text  # a GET publishes nothing\n    assert act(admin, url, \"publish\").status_code == 303\n    assert \"Status: Published\" in admin.get(url).text\n\n\ndef test_officer_cannot_publish_even_by_direct_post(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    url = post_url(create_post(editor))\n    assert editor.get(url + \"/publish\").status_code == 403\n    assert act(editor, url, \"publish\").status_code == 403\n    assert \"Status: Draft\" in editor.get(url).text\n\n\ndef test_president_unpublishes_a_published_post(client_as):\n\"\"\"T02: Officers write and edit their own Post drafts. Seam 1: the HTTP app.\"\"\"\nimport re\n\nfrom tests.conftest import csrf_of\n\n\ndef create_post(c, title=\"Meeting announcement\", body=\"Come to **Oden 110**.\"):\n    token = csrf_of(c, \"/admin/posts/new\")\n    return c.post(\"/admin/posts\", data={\"title\": title, \"body\": body, \"csrf_token\": token},\n                  follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef post_url(response) -> str:\n    assert response.status_code == 303\n    return response.headers[\"location\"]\n\n\ndef test_officer_creates_a_draft_they_own(client_as):\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    url = post_url(create_post(c))\n    assert re.fullmatch(r\"/admin/posts/\\d+\", url)\n    page = c.get(url)\n    assert page.status_code == 200\n    assert \"Meeting announcement\" in page.text\n    assert \"Draft\" in page.text\n    listing = c.get(\"/admin/posts\").text\n    assert \"Meeting announcement\" in listing\n    assert \"editor@example.test\" in listing\n\n\ndef test_slug_is_made_once_unique_and_survives_title_edits(client_as):\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    first = post_url(create_post(c, title=\"Spring Stock Pitch!\"))\n    second = post_url(create_post(c, title=\"Spring Stock Pitch!\"))\n    assert \"spring-stock-pitch\" in c.get(first).text\n    assert \"spring-stock-pitch-2\" in c.get(second).text\n    token = csrf_of(c, first)\n    c.post(first, data={\"title\": \"Totally different\", \"body\": \"x\", \"csrf_token\": token})\n    edited = c.get(first).text\n    assert \"Totally different\" in edited\n#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Create demo data so a grader (and you) can use the CMS immediately.\n\n    uv run python scripts/seed_demo.py\n\nT01 creates the users. Later tickets extend this so it also creates:\n  - (done in T01) one admin and one editor, passwords read from the environment\n  - a few posts and pages, at least one draft and one published\n\nThe rubric expects this to run clean on a fresh clone with .env.example values\n(item E4), because the database itself is never committed.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport os\nimport sys\n\n\ndef main() -> int:\n    admin_pw = os.environ.get(\"CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD\")\n    editor_pw = os.environ.get(\"CMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD\")\n    if not admin_pw or not editor_pw:\n        print(\"Set CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD and CMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD in .env \"\n              \"(copy .env.example).\")\n        return 1\n\n    # Local import so the script runs from a plain `uv run python scripts/...`.\n    from app import settings\n    from app.seed import seed_users\n\n    created = seed_users(settings.DATABASE_PATH, {\n        \"admin\": (os.environ.get(\"CMS_ADMIN_EMAIL\", \"admin@example.test\"), admin_pw),\n        \"editor\": (os.environ.get(\"CMS_EDITOR_EMAIL\", \"editor@example.test\"), editor_pw),\n    })\n    print(f\"Users created: {', '.join(created) or 'none (already present)'}\")\n    # TODO (later tickets): demo posts and pages, one draft and one published.\n    return 0\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    sys.exit(main())\n\"\"\"The `cms` command: serve, publish, deploy.\n\n    uv run cms serve      # admin console + public preview at http://localhost:8000\n    uv run cms publish    # render site/ from published content\n    uv run cms deploy     # push site/ to the gh-pages branch (Pages serves it)\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport argparse\nimport subprocess\nimport sys\n\nfrom app import settings\nfrom app.publish import render_site\n\n\ndef main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog=\"cms\")\n    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest=\"command\", required=True)\n    serve = sub.add_parser(\"serve\", help=\"run the admin console locally\")\n    serve.add_argument(\"--port\", type=int, default=8000)\n    serve.add_argument(\"--reload\", action=\"store_true\", default=True)\n    sub.add_parser(\"publish\", help=\"render site/ from published content\")\n    deploy = sub.add_parser(\"deploy\", help=\"push site/ to gh-pages\")\n    deploy.add_argument(\"--message\", default=\"Publish site\")\n    args = parser.parse_args(argv)\n\n    if args.command == \"serve\":\n        try:\n            settings.require_safe_secret_key()\n        except RuntimeError as problem:\n            print(problem)\n            return 1\n        import uvicorn\n\n        uvicorn.run(\"app.main:app\", port=args.port, reload=args.reload)\n        return 0\n\n    if args.command == \"publish\":\n        out = render_site()\n        print(f\"Wrote {out}. Preview it with:  python3 -m http.server -d {out} 8001\")\n        return 0\n\n    if args.command == \"deploy\":\n        if not settings.SITE.exists():\n            print(\"site/ does not exist yet — run `uv run cms publish` first.\")\n            return 1\n        result = subprocess.run(\n            [sys.executable, \"-m\", \"ghp_import\", \"-n\", \"-p\", \"-m\", args.message,\n             str(settings.SITE)],\n        )\n        if result.returncode == 0:\n            print(\"Pushed to gh-pages. Settings -> Pages -> Deploy from a branch -> \"\n                  \"gh-pages / root, then wait up to 10 minutes.\")\n        return result.returncode\n    return 1\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    raise SystemExit(main())\n\"\"\"Render the public site into site/ as plain HTML.\n\nT00 publishes a placeholder home page. As you build content types, extend\nrender_site() to write one file per published item. Two rules the rubric checks:\n\n  1. Only PUBLISHED content is written here. A draft that reaches site/ is a bug.\n  2. Every href and src is RELATIVE (\"style.css\", \"posts/x.html\"), never\n     root-absolute (\"/style.css\"), because Pages serves this from a subfolder.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport shutil\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom jinja2 import Environment, FileSystemLoader, select_autoescape\n\nfrom app import settings\n\nCSS = \"\"\"/* Minimal starter styles — make them yours. */\n:root { color-scheme: light dark; }\nbody { font: 16px/1.6 system-ui, sans-serif; margin: 0 auto; max-width: 42rem; padding: 1rem; }\nheader a { font-weight: 700; text-decoration: none; }\nmain { margin-block: 2rem; }\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef environment() -> Environment:\n    return Environment(\n        loader=FileSystemLoader(str(settings.TEMPLATES)),\n        autoescape=select_autoescape([\"html\"]),\n    )\n\n\ndef render_site(out: Path | None = None) -> Path:\n    out = out or settings.SITE\n    if out.exists():\n        shutil.rmtree(out)\n %}","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"ef75a911-e6fa-47ae-bc6a-b301b23d4958","timestamp":"2026-10-15T03:58:07.108Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"\"\"\"T03: Presidents publish, unpublish and delete Posts. Seam 1: the HTTP app.\"\"\"\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n\nfrom tests.conftest import csrf_of, sign_in\nfrom tests.test_t02_posts import create_post, post_url\n\n\ndef president_for(editor) -> TestClient:\n    \"\"\"A President on the same app and database as `editor`.\"\"\"\n    admin = TestClient(editor.app)\n    sign_in(admin, \"admin@example.test\", \"test-admin-pw\")\n    return admin\n\n\ndef act(c, url, action, **extra):\n    token = csrf_of(c, url)\n    return c.post(f\"{url}/{action}\", data={\"csrf_token\": token, **extra},\n                  follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef published_post(editor, title=\"Pitch: NVDA\"):\n    url = post_url(create_post(editor, title=title))\n    admin = president_for(editor)\n    assert act(admin, url, \"publish\").status_code == 303\n    assert \"Published\" in admin.get(url).text\n    return url, admin\n\n\ndef test_president_publishes_a_draft_after_a_confirm(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    url = post_url(create_post(editor))\n    admin = president_for(editor)\n    confirm = admin.get(url + \"/publish\")\n    assert confirm.status_code == 200\n    assert \"Publish this?\" in confirm.text\n    assert 'name=\"csrf_token\"' in confirm.text\n    assert \"Status: Draft\" in admin.get(url).text  # a GET publishes nothing\n    assert act(admin, url, \"publish\").status_code == 303\n    assert \"Status: Published\" in admin.get(url).text\n\n\ndef test_officer_cannot_publish_even_by_direct_post(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    url = post_url(create_post(editor))\n    assert editor.get(url + \"/publish\").status_code == 403\n    assert act(editor, url, \"publish\").status_code == 403\n    assert \"Status: Draft\" in editor.get(url).text\n\n\ndef test_president_unpublishes_a_published_post(client_as):\n\"\"\"T02: Officers write and edit their own Post drafts. Seam 1: the HTTP app.\"\"\"\nimport re\n\nfrom tests.conftest import csrf_of\n\n\ndef create_post(c, title=\"Meeting announcement\", body=\"Come to **Oden 110**.\"):\n    token = csrf_of(c, \"/admin/posts/new\")\n    return c.post(\"/admin/posts\", data={\"title\": title, \"body\": body, \"csrf_token\": token},\n                  follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef post_url(response) -> str:\n    assert response.status_code == 303\n    return response.headers[\"location\"]\n\n\ndef test_officer_creates_a_draft_they_own(client_as):\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    url = post_url(create_post(c))\n    assert re.fullmatch(r\"/admin/posts/\\d+\", url)\n    page = c.get(url)\n    assert page.status_code == 200\n    assert \"Meeting announcement\" in page.text\n    assert \"Draft\" in page.text\n    listing = c.get(\"/admin/posts\").text\n    assert \"Meeting announcement\" in listing\n    assert \"editor@example.test\" in listing\n\n\ndef test_slug_is_made_once_unique_and_survives_title_edits(client_as):\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    first = post_url(create_post(c, title=\"Spring Stock Pitch!\"))\n    second = post_url(create_post(c, title=\"Spring Stock Pitch!\"))\n    assert \"spring-stock-pitch\" in c.get(first).text\n    assert \"spring-stock-pitch-2\" in c.get(second).text\n    token = csrf_of(c, first)\n    c.post(first, data={\"title\": \"Totally different\", \"body\": \"x\", \"csrf_token\": token})\n    edited = c.get(first).text\n    assert \"Totally different\" in edited\n#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Create demo data so a grader (and you) can use the CMS immediately.\n\n    uv run python scripts/seed_demo.py\n\nT01 creates the users. Later tickets extend this so it also creates:\n  - (done in T01) one admin and one editor, passwords read from the environment\n  - a few posts and pages, at least one draft and one published\n\nThe rubric expects this to run clean on a fresh clone with .env.example values\n(item E4), because the database itself is never committed.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport os\nimport sys\n\n\ndef main() -> int:\n    admin_pw = os.environ.get(\"CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD\")\n    editor_pw = os.environ.get(\"CMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD\")\n    if not admin_pw or not editor_pw:\n        print(\"Set CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD and CMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD in .env \"\n              \"(copy .env.example).\")\n        return 1\n\n    # Local import so the script runs from a plain `uv run python scripts/...`.\n    from app import settings\n    from app.seed import seed_users\n\n    created = seed_users(settings.DATABASE_PATH, {\n        \"admin\": (os.environ.get(\"CMS_ADMIN_EMAIL\", \"admin@example.test\"), admin_pw),\n        \"editor\": (os.environ.get(\"CMS_EDITOR_EMAIL\", \"editor@example.test\"), editor_pw),\n    })\n    print(f\"Users created: {', '.join(created) or 'none (already present)'}\")\n    # TODO (later tickets): demo posts and pages, one draft and one published.\n    return 0\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    sys.exit(main())\n\"\"\"The `cms` command: serve, publish, deploy.\n\n    uv run cms serve      # admin console + public preview at http://localhost:8000\n    uv run cms publish    # render site/ from published content\n    uv run cms deploy     # push site/ to the gh-pages branch (Pages serves it)\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport argparse\nimport subprocess\nimport sys\n\nfrom app import settings\nfrom app.publish import render_site\n\n\ndef main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog=\"cms\")\n    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest=\"command\", required=True)\n    serve = sub.add_parser(\"serve\", help=\"run the admin console locally\")\n    serve.add_argument(\"--port\", type=int, default=8000)\n    serve.add_argument(\"--reload\", action=\"store_true\", default=True)\n    sub.add_parser(\"publish\", help=\"render site/ from published content\")\n    deploy = sub.add_parser(\"deploy\", help=\"push site/ to gh-pages\")\n    deploy.add_argument(\"--message\", default=\"Publish site\")\n    args = parser.parse_args(argv)\n\n    if args.command == \"serve\":\n        try:\n            settings.require_safe_secret_key()\n        except RuntimeError as problem:\n            print(problem)\n            return 1\n        import uvicorn\n\n        uvicorn.run(\"app.main:app\", port=args.port, reload=args.reload)\n        return 0\n\n    if args.command == \"publish\":\n        out = render_site()\n        print(f\"Wrote {out}. Preview it with:  python3 -m http.server -d {out} 8001\")\n        return 0\n\n    if args.command == \"deploy\":\n        if not settings.SITE.exists():\n            print(\"site/ does not exist yet — run `uv run cms publish` first.\")\n            return 1\n        result = subprocess.run(\n            [sys.executable, \"-m\", \"ghp_import\", \"-n\", \"-p\", \"-m\", args.message,\n             str(settings.SITE)],\n        )\n        if result.returncode == 0:\n            print(\"Pushed to gh-pages. Settings -> Pages -> Deploy from a branch -> \"\n                  \"gh-pages / root, then wait up to 10 minutes.\")\n        return result.returncode\n    return 1\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    raise SystemExit(main())\n\"\"\"Render the public site into site/ as plain HTML.\n\nT00 publishes a placeholder home page. As you build content types, extend\nrender_site() to write one file per published item. Two rules the rubric checks:\n\n  1. Only PUBLISHED content is written here. A draft that reaches site/ is a bug.\n  2. Every href and src is RELATIVE (\"style.css\", \"posts/x.html\"), never\n     root-absolute (\"/style.css\"), because Pages serves this from a subfolder.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport shutil\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom jinja2 import Environment, FileSystemLoader, select_autoescape\n\nfrom app import settings\n\nCSS = \"\"\"/* Minimal starter styles — make them yours. */\n:root { color-scheme: light dark; }\nbody { font: 16px/1.6 system-ui, sans-serif; margin: 0 auto; max-width: 42rem; padding: 1rem; }\nheader a { font-weight: 700; text-decoration: none; }\nmain { margin-block: 2rem; }\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef environment() -> Environment:\n    return Environment(\n        loader=FileSystemLoader(str(settings.TEMPLATES)),\n        autoescape=select_autoescape([\"html\"]),\n    )\n\n\ndef render_site(out: Path | None = None) -> Path:\n    out = out or settings.SITE\n    if out.exists():\n        shutil.rmtree(out)\n    out.mkdir(parents=True)\n    env = environment()\n    (out / \"style.css\").write_text(CSS)\n    (out / \"index.html\").write_text(\n        env.get_template(\"public/home.html\").render(\n            title=settings.SITE_TITLE, items=[], css_path=\"style.css\",\n            home_path=\"index.html\",\n        )\n    )\n    return out\n    assert titles_in_order(page, [\"Older post\", \"Newer post\"]) == [\"Newer post\", \"Older post\"]\n    for label, n in ((\"All\", 2), (\"Published\", 1), (\"Draft\", 1)):\n        assert re.search(rf'<a href=\"[^\"]*status=\\w+[^\"]*\">{label} title=\"Live one\"))\n    create_post(editor, title=\"Draft one\")\n    act(admin, live, \"publish\")\n    published = admin.get(\"/admin/posts?status=published\").text\n    assert \"Live one\" in published and \"Draft one\" not in published\n    drafts = admin.get(\"/admin/posts?status=draft\").text\n    assert \"Draft one\" in drafts and \"Live one\" not in drafts\n    both = admin.get(\"/admin/posts?status=all\").text\n    assert \"Live one\" in both and \"Draft one\" in both\n    assert \"Live one\" in admin.get(\"/admin/posts?status=bogus\").text  # unknown means All\n\n\ndef test_type_filter_limits_to_posts_or_pages_without_breaking(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    admin = president_for(editor)\n    create_post(editor, title=\"A post\")\n    assert \"A post\" in admin.get(\"/admin/posts?type=post\").text\n    pages = admin.get(\"/admin/posts?type=page\")\n    assert pages.status_code == 200\n    assert \"A post\" not in pages.text\n    assert admin.get(\"/admin/posts?type=bogus\").status_code == 200\n\n\ndef test_officer_list_and_counts_include_only_their_own_posts(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    admin = president_for(editor)\n    create_post(editor, title=\"Mine\")\n    create_post(admin, title=\"President only\")\n    listing = editor.get(\"/admin/posts\").text\n    assert \"Mine\" in listing and \\(1\\)</a>\", listing)\n    assert \"President only\" not in editor.get(\"/admin/posts?status=draft\").text\n\n\ndef test_officer_published_count_and_filters_cover_only_their_posts(client_as):\n {"type":"api-request-blob","sessionId":"2ccec162-c0eb-4cb8-a952-47bc94d99a51","hash":"11bce73b26d8e7a3a687cc1adc00bf1da7cdebab8dc1605e5cd67240254a36b3","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"tool_result","tool_use_id":"toolu_0163Wk8DVoi55T5sBtdS4A3b","content":"Launching skill: tdd"},{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /Users/biswas1/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/.claude/skills/tdd\n\n# Test-Driven Development\n\nTDD is the red → green loop. This skill is the reference that makes that loop produce tests worth keeping: what a good test is, where tests go, the anti-patterns, and the rules of the loop. Every section applies on every cycle: consult them before and during the loop, not after.\n\nWhen exploring the codebase, read `CONTEXT.md` (if it exists) so test names and interface vocabulary match the project's domain language, and respect ADRs in the area you're touching.\n\n## What a good test is\n\nTests verify behavior through public interfaces, not implementation details. Code can change entirely; tests shouldn't. A good test reads like a specification: \"user can checkout with valid cart\" tells you exactly what capability exists, and it survives refactors because it doesn't care about internal structure.\n\nSee [tests.md](tests.md) for examples and [mocking.md](mocking.md) for mocking guidelines.\n\n## Seams: where tests go\n\nA **seam** is the public boundary you test at: the interface where you observe behavior without reaching inside. Tests live at seams, never against internals.\n\n**Test only at pre-agreed seams.** Before writing any test, write down the seams under test and confirm them with the user. No test is written at an unconfirmed seam. You can't test everything, so agreeing the seams up front is how testing effort lands on the critical paths and complex logic instead of every edge case.\n\nAsk: \"What's the public interface, and which seams should we test?\" Give each proposed seam a one-line note on what it catches and what it misses.\n\nWhen the shape of that interface is itself in question (how deep the module is, where the seam belongs, what the interface should expose), call the Skill tool with \"codebase-design\" for the vocabulary. It is the shared source of the module, interface, depth, seam, adapter, leverage and locality terms, and it is a reference to consult, not a session to run.\n\n## Anti-patterns\n\n- **Implementation-coupled**: mocks internal collaborators, tests private methods, or verifies through a side channel (querying the database instead of using the interface). The tell: the test breaks when you refactor but behavior hasn't changed.\n- **Tautological**: the assertion recomputes the expected value the way the code does (`expect(add(a, b)).toBe(a + b)`, a snapshot derived by hand the same way, a constant asserted equal to itself), so it passes by construction and can never disagree with the code. Expected values must come from an independent source of truth: a known-good literal, a worked example, the spec.\n- **Horizontal slicing**: writing all tests first, then all implementation. Bulk tests verify _imagined_ behavior: you test the _shape_ of things rather than user-facing behavior, the tests go insensitive to real changes, and you commit to test structure before understanding the implementation. Work in **vertical slices** instead: one test → one implementation → repeat, each test a **tracer bullet** that responds to what the last cycle taught you.\n\n## Rules of the loop\n\n- **Red before green.** Write the failing test first, then only enough code to pass it. Don't anticipate future tests or add speculative features.\n- **One slice at a time.** One seam, one test, one minimal implementation per cycle.\n- **Refactoring is not part of the loop.** It belongs to the review stage (see the `code-review` skill), not the red → green implementation cycle.\n"}]}}

{"parentUuid":"a2967737-6246-43bd-b41b-1c051c4c8fe5","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"c231bb73-8d53-4531-b686-e572c28d9639","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /Users/biswas1/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/.claude/skills/tdd\n\n# Test-Driven Development\n\nTDD is the red → green loop. This skill is the reference that makes that loop produce tests worth keeping: what a good test is, where tests go, the anti-patterns, and the rules of the loop. Every section applies on every cycle: consult them before and during the loop, not after.\n\nWhen exploring the codebase, read `CONTEXT.md` (if it exists) so test names and interface vocabulary match the project's domain language, and respect ADRs in the area you're touching.\n\n## What a good test is\n\nTests verify behavior through public interfaces, not implementation details. Code can change entirely; tests shouldn't. A good test reads like a specification: \"user can checkout with valid cart\" tells you exactly what capability exists, and it survives refactors because it doesn't care about internal structure.\n\nSee [tests.md](tests.md) for examples and [mocking.md](mocking.md) for mocking guidelines.\n\n## Seams: where tests go\n\nA **seam** is the public boundary you test at: the interface where you observe behavior without reaching inside. Tests live at seams, never against internals.\n\n**Test only at pre-agreed seams.** Before writing any test, write down the seams under test and confirm them with the user. No test is written at an unconfirmed seam. You can't test everything, so agreeing the seams up front is how testing effort lands on the critical paths and complex logic instead of every edge case.\n\nAsk: \"What's the public interface, and which seams should we test?\" Give each proposed seam a one-line note on what it catches and what it misses.\n\nWhen the shape of that interface is itself in question (how deep the module is, where the seam belongs, what the interface should expose), call the Skill tool with \"codebase-design\" for the vocabulary. It is the shared source of the module, interface, depth, seam, adapter, leverage and locality terms, and it is a reference to consult, not a session to run.\n\n## Anti-patterns\n\n- **Implementation-coupled**: mocks internal collaborators, tests private methods, or verifies through a side channel (querying the database instead of using the interface). The tell: the test breaks when you refactor but behavior hasn't changed.\n- **Tautological**: the assertion recomputes the expected value the way the code does (`expect(add(a, b)).toBe(a + b)`, a snapshot derived by hand the same way, a constant asserted equal to itself), so it passes by construction and can never disagree with the code. Expected values must come from an independent source of truth: a known-good literal, a worked example, the spec.\n- **Horizontal slicing**: writing all tests first, then all implementation. Bulk tests verify _imagined_ behavior: you test the _shape_ of things rather than user-facing behavior, the tests go insensitive to real changes, and you commit to test structure before understanding the implementation. Work in **vertical slices** instead: one test → one implementation → repeat, each test a **tracer bullet** that responds to what the last cycle taught you.\n\n## Rules of the loop\n\n- **Red before green.** Write the failing test first, then only enough code to pass it. Don't anticipate future tests or add speculative features.\n- **One slice at a time.** One seam, one test, one minimal implementation per cycle.\n- **Refactoring is not part of the loop.** It belongs to the review stage (see the `code-review` skill), not the red → green implementation cycle.\n"}]},"isMeta":true,"turnCompanion":true,"uuid":"eeee4499-f0de-4f19-b757-c58e2d07c143","timestamp":"2026-10-15T03:58:09.086Z","sourceToolUseID":"toolu_0163Wk8DVoi55T5sBtdS4A3b","session_id":"2ccec162-c0eb-4cb8-a952-47bc94d99a51","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/biswas1/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"2ccec162-c0eb-4cb8-a952-47bc94d99a51","version":"2.1.300","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"198242fd-2943-4ce3-8327-bff1173da3d8","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011Cg3MNGjpQywN6WKT6DwND","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01PxGMikG3JSQJ3HcMQC5cDy","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/biswas1/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/tests/test_t05_pages.py","content":"\"\"\"T05: President-only Pages. Seam 1: the HTTP app.\"\"\"\nimport re\n\nfrom app import db, pages\nfrom tests.conftest import csrf_of\nfrom tests.test_t03_president_control import act, president_for\n\n\ndef create_page(c, title=\"About\", body=\"We are KCFC.\", order=\"2\"):\n    token = csrf_of(c, \"/admin/pages/new\")\n    return c.post(\"/admin/pages\", data={\"title\": title, \"body\": body, \"nav_order\": order,\n                                        \"csrf_token\": token}, follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef page_url(response) -> str:\n    assert response.status_code == 303\n    return response.headers[\"location\"]\n\n\ndef test_president_creates_edits_and_sees_a_page(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    url = page_url(create_page(admin))\n    assert re.fullmatch(r\"/admin/pages/\\d+\", url)\n    html = admin.get(url).text\n    assert \"About\" in html and \"Status: Draft\" in html and \"about\" in html\n    token = csrf_of(admin, url)\n    admin.post(url, data={\"title\": \"About us\", \"body\": \"New\", \"nav_order\": \"5\",\n                          \"csrf_token\": token}, follow_redirects=False)\n    html = admin.get(url).text\n    assert \"About us\" in html and 'value=\"5\"' in html\n\n\ndef test_slug_is_generated_once_and_editable_by_president(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    url = page_url(create_page(admin, title=\"Join Us\"))\n    token = csrf_of(admin, url)\n    admin.post(url, data={\"title\": \"Join the club\", \"body\": \"\", \"nav_order\": \"1\",\n                          \"csrf_token\": token})\n    assert 'value=\"join-us\"' in admin.get(url).text\n    admin.post(url, data={\"title\": \"Join the club\", \"body\": \"\", \"nav_order\": \"1\",\n                          \"slug\": \"Get Involved\", \"csrf_token\": token})\n    assert 'value=\"get-involved\"' in admin.get(url).text\n\n\ndef test_navigation_order_is_stored_on_the_page(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    url = page_url(create_page(admin, order=\"7\"))\n    conn = db.connect(admin.app.state.database)\n    pid = int(url.rsplit(\"/\", 1)[1])\n    assert pages.get_page(conn, pid).nav_order == 7\n    conn.close()\n\n\ndef test_president_publishes_unpublishes_and_deletes_with_confirms(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    url = page_url(create_page(admin))\n    assert \"Publish this?\" in admin.get(url + \"/publish\").text\n    assert \"Status: Draft\" in admin.get(url).text\n    assert act(admin, url, \"publish\").status_code == 303\n    assert \"Status: Published\" in admin.get(url).text\n    assert \"Are you sure you want to unpublish this?\" in admin.get(url + \"/unpublish\").text\n    act(admin, url, \"unpublish\")\n    assert \"Status: Draft\" in admin.get(url).text\n    assert \"Are you sure\" in admin.get(url + \"/delete\").text\n    assert act(admin, url, \"delete\").status_code == 303\n    assert admin.get(url).status_code == 404\n\n\ndef test_officer_is_refused_on_every_page_url_get_and_post(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    admin = president_for(editor)\n    url = page_url(create_page(admin))\n    token = csrf_of(editor, \"/admin/posts/new\")\n    for path in (\"/admin/pages\", \"/admin/pages/new\", url, url + \"/publish\",\n                 url + \"/unpublish\", url + \"/delete\"):\n        assert editor.get(path).status_code in (302, 303, 403), path\n        r = editor.post(path, data={\"csrf_token\": token, \"title\": \"x\"}, follow_redirects=False)\n        assert r.status_code in (302, 303, 403), path\n    assert \"Status: Draft\" in admin.get(url).text\n    assert admin.get(url).status_code == 200  # still there\n\n\ndef test_officer_sees_no_pages_link_or_pages_in_content_list(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    admin = president_for(editor)\n    create_page(admin, title=\"Secret page\")\n    for path in (\"/admin\", \"/admin/posts\", \"/admin/posts?type=page\"):\n        html = editor.get(path).text\n        assert \"/admin/pages\" not in html and \"Secret page\" not in html, path\n    assert President-only Pages. Seam 1: the HTTP app.\"\"\"\nimport re\n\nfrom app import db, pages\nfrom tests.conftest import csrf_of\nfrom tests.test_t03_president_control import act, president_for\n\n\ndef create_page(c, title=\"About\", body=\"We are KCFC.\", order=\"2\"):\n    token = csrf_of(c, \"/admin/pages/new\")\n    return c.post(\"/admin/pages\", data={\"title\": title, \"body\": body, \"nav_order\": order,\n                                        \"csrf_token\": token}, follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef page_url(response) -> str:\n    assert response.status_code == 303\n    return response.headers[\"location\"]\n\n\ndef test_president_creates_edits_and_sees_a_page(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    url = page_url(create_page(admin))\n    assert re.fullmatch(r\"/admin/pages/\\d+\", url)\n    html = admin.get(url).text\n    assert \"About\" in html and \"Status: Draft\" in html and \"about\" in html\n    token = csrf_of(admin, url)\n    admin.post(url, data={\"title\": \"About us\", \"body\": \"New\", \"nav_order\": \"5\",\n                          \"csrf_token\": token}, follow_redirects=False)\n    html = admin.get(url).text\n    assert \"About us\" in html and 'value=\"5\"' in html\n\n\ndef test_slug_is_generated_once_and_editable_by_president(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    url = page_url(create_page(admin, title=\"Join Us\"))\n    token = csrf_of(admin, url)\n    admin.post(url, data={\"title\": \"Join the club\", \"body\": \"\", \"nav_order\": \"1\",\n                          \"csrf_token\": token})\n    assert 'value=\"join-us\"' in admin.get(url).text\n    admin.post(url, data={\"title\": \"Join the club\", \"body\": \"\", \"nav_order\": \"1\",\n                          \"slug\": \"Get Involved\", \"csrf_token\": token})\n    assert 'value=\"get-involved\"' in admin.get(url).text\n\n\ndef test_navigation_order_is_stored_on_the_page(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    url = page_url(create_page(admin, order=\"7\"))\n    conn = db.connect(admin.app.state.database)\n    pid = int(url.rsplit(\"/\", 1)[1])\n    assert pages.get_page(conn, pid).nav_order == 7\n    conn.close()\n\n\ndef test_president_publishes_unpublishes_and_deletes_with_confirms(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    url = page_url(create_page(admin))\n    assert \"Publish this?\" in admin.get(url + \"/publish\").text\n    assert \"Status: Draft\" in admin.get(url).text\n    assert act(admin, url, \"publish\").status_code == 303\n    assert \"Status: Published\" in admin.get(url).text\n    assert \"Are you sure you want to unpublish this?\" in admin.get(url + \"/unpublish\").text\n    act(admin, url, \"unpublish\")\n    assert \"Status: Draft\" in admin.get(url).text\n    assert \"Are you sure\" in admin.get(url + \"/delete\").text\n    assert act(admin, url, \"delete\").status_code == 303\n    assert admin.get(url).status_code == 404\n\n\ndef test_officer_is_refused_on_every_page_url_get_and_post(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    admin = president_for(editor)\n    url = page_url(create_page(admin))\n    token = csrf_of(editor, \"/admin/posts/new\")\n    for path in (\"/admin/pages\", \"/admin/pages/new\", url, url + \"/publish\",\n                 url + \"/unpublish\", url + \"/delete\"):\n        assert editor.get(path).status_code in (302, 303, 403), path\n        r = editor.post(path, data={\"csrf_token\": token, \"title\": \"x\"}, follow_redirects=False)\n        assert r.status_code in (302, 303, 403), path\n    assert \"Status: Draft\" in admin.get(url).text\n    assert admin.get(url).status_code == 200  # still there\n\n\ndef test_officer_sees_no_pages_link_or_pages_in_content_list(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    admin = president_for(editor)\n    create_page(admin, title=\"Secret page\")\n    for path in (\"/admin\", \"/admin/posts\", \"/admin/posts?type=page\"):\n        html = editor.get(path).text\n        assert \"/admin/pages\" not in html and \"Secret page\" not in html, path\n    assert \"/admin/pages\" in admin.get(\"/admin\").text\n\n\ndef test_content_list_includes_pages_for_president_and_type_filter(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    admin = president_for(editor)\n    create_page(admin, title=\"Leadership\")\n    editor_post = editor.post(\"/admin/posts\", data={\n        \"title\": \"A post\", \"body\": \"\", \"csrf_token\": csrf_of(editor, \"/admin/posts/new\")})\n    assert editor_post.status_code in (200, 303)\n    assert \"Leadership\" in admin.get(\"/admin/posts\").text\n    only_pages = admin.get(\"/admin/posts?type=page\").text\n    assert \"Leadership\" in only_pages and \"A post\" not in only_pages\n    only_posts = admin.get(\"/admin/posts?type=post\").text\n    assert \"A post\" in only_posts and \"Leadership\" not in only_posts\n    assert re.search(r\">All \\(2\\)</a>\", admin.get(\"/admin/posts\").text)\n\n\ndef test_seed_creates_the_six_pages_as_drafts_once(make_app):\n    app = make_app()\n    for _ in range(2):\n        pages.seed_pages(app.state.database)\n    conn = db.connect(app.state.database)\n    found = [(p.title, p.status) for p in pages.list_pages(conn)]\n    conn.close()\n    assert sorted(found) == sorted((t, \"draft\") for t in (\n        \"Homepage\", \"About\", \"Leadership\", \"Analyst Program\", \"Join\", \"Recruiting\"))\n"}},"apiBlockIndex":2,"requestId":"req_011Cg3MNGTTCq8bSYhYrYpBB","requestRef":"7ecf1fe9-f910-46c9-a658-2ba694e403d7","requestedModel":"claude-sonnet-5-5","attributionSkill":"tdd","type":"assistant","uuid":"e338ae21-12b2-4156-a307-c643361251aa","timestamp":"2026-10-15T03:58:23.455Z","advisorModel":"claude-opus-5-5","effort":"medium","perTurnEffort":"medium","session_id":"2ccec162-c0eb-4cb8-a952-47bc94d99a51","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/biswas1/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"2ccec162-c0eb-4cb8-a952-47bc94d99a51","version":"2.1.300","gitBranch":"main"}

Like a Post, plus a navigation order; Admin-only.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\nimport time\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom app import db\nfrom app.posts import DRAFT, PUBLISHED, slugify\n\nSEED_TITLES = (\"Homepage\", \"About\", \"Leadership\", \"Analyst Program\", \"Join\", \"Recruiting\")\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass Page:\n    id: int\n    title: str\n    slug: str\n    body: str\n    status: str\n    nav_order: int\n    created_at: float\n    updated_at: float\n\n\ndef _row(row: sqlite3.Row) -> Page:\n    return Page(row[\"id\"], row[\"title\"], row[\"slug\"], row[\"body\"], row[\"status\"],\n                row[\"nav_order\"], row[\"created_at\"], row[\"updated_at\"])\n\n\ndef _unique_slug(conn: sqlite3.Connection, title: str) -> str:\n    base = slugify(title)\n    slug, n = base, 1\n    while conn.execute(\"SELECT 1 FROM pages WHERE slug = ?\", (slug,)).fetchone():\n        n += 1\n        slug = f\"{base}-{n}\"\n    return slug\n\n\ndef create_page(conn: sqlite3.Connection, title: str, body: str, nav_order: int,\n                now: float) -> int:\n    \"\"\"New Pages are always Drafts.\"\"\"\n    with conn:\n        cur = conn.execute(\n            \"INSERT INTO pages (title, slug, body, status, nav_order, created_at, updated_at)\"\n            \" VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n            (title, _unique_slug(conn, title), body, DRAFT, nav_order, now, now))\n    return cur.lastrowid\n\n\ndef get_page(conn: sqlite3.Connection, page_id: int) -> Page | None:\n    row = conn.execute(\"SELECT * FROM pages WHERE id = ?\", (page_id,)).fetchone()\n    return _row(row) if row else None\n\n\ndef list_pages(conn: sqlite3.Connection, status: str | None = None) -> list[Page]:\n    \"\"\"Newest first, optionally one status.\"\"\"\n    where, args = (\"\", ()) if status is None else (\" WHERE status = ?\", (status,))\n    rows = conn.execute(\"SELECT * FROM pages\" + where + \" ORDER BY created_at DESC, id DESC\",\n                        args).fetchall()\n    return [_row(r) for r in rows]\n\n\ndef count_by_status(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> dict[str, int]:\n    found = {r[\"status\"]: r[\"n\"] for r in\n             conn.execute(\"SELECT status, COUNT(*) AS n FROM pages GROUP BY status\")}\n    return {DRAFT: found.get(DRAFT, 0), PUBLISHED: found.get(PUBLISHED, 0)}\n\n\ndef update_page(conn: sqlite3.Connection, page_id: int, title: str, body: str,\n                nav_order: int, now: float, slug: str | None = None) -> None:\n    \"\"\"The slug changes only when a normalised `slug` is passed.\"\"\"\n    with conn:\n        conn.execute(\n            \"UPDATE pages SET title = ?, body = ?, nav_order = ?, updated_at = ?,\"\n            \" slug = COALESCE(?, slug) WHERE id = ?\",\n            (title, body, nav_order, now, slug, page_id))\n\n\ndef set_status(conn: sqlite3.Connection, page_id: int, status: str, now: float) -> None:\n    with conn:\n        conn.execute(\"UPDATE pages SET status = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?\",\n                     (status, now, page_id))\n\n\ndef slug_taken(conn: sqlite3.Connection, slug: str, page_id: int) -> bool:\n    return conn.execute(\"SELECT 1 FROM pages WHERE slug = ? AND id != ?\",\n                        (slug, page_id)).fetchone() is not None\n\n\ndef delete_page(conn: sqlite3.Connection, page_id: int) -> None:\n    with conn:\n        conn.execute(\"DELETE FROM pages WHERE id = ?\", (page_id,))\n\n\ndef seed_pages(database: Path) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"Create the club's named Pages as Drafts; existing titles are left alone.\"\"\"\n    db.init_db(database)\n    conn = db.connect(database)\n    existing = {p.title for p in list_pages(conn)}\n    created = []\n    for order, title in enumerate(SEED_TITLES, start=1):\n        if title not in existing:\n            create_page(conn, title, \"\", order, time.time())\n            created.append(title)\n    conn.close()\n    return created\nEOF\ncat > app/routes/pages.py <<'EOF'\n\"\"\"Console routes for Pages. President-only: every route is behind the admin guard.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Form, HTTPException, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\n\nfrom app import auth, db, pages\nfrom app.markdown import render_markdown\nfrom app.posts import DRAFT, PUBLISHED, slugify\nfrom app.templating import templates\n\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/admin/pages\", dependencies=[Depends(auth.require_role(\"admin\"))])\n\n\ndef _page(conn, page_id: int) -> pages.Page:\n    page = pages.get_page(conn, page_id)\n    if page is None:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=\"No such Page\")\n    return page\n\n\ndef _int(value: str) -> int:\n    try:\n        return int(value.strip() or 0)\n    except ValueError:\n        return 0\n\n\ndef _editor(request, user, page, title, body, nav_order, error=None, status_code=200):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/page_edit.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Edit Page\" if page else \"New Page\", \"user\": user, \"page\": page,\n         \"form_title\": title, \"form_body\": body, \"form_nav_order\": nav_order,\n         \"preview_html\": render_markdown(body), \"error\": error},\n        status_code=status_code)\n\n\n@router.get(\"\")\ndef page_list():\n    return RedirectResponse(\"/admin/posts?type=page\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/new\")\ndef page_new(request: Request, user=Depends(auth.require_user)):\n    return _editor(request, user, None, \"\", \"\", 0)\n\n\n@router.post(\"\")\ndef page_create(request: Request, title: str = Form(\"\"), body: str = Form(\"\"),\n                nav_order: str = Form(\"0\"), user=Depends(auth.require_user),\n                conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    title = title.strip()\n    if not title:\n        return _editor(request, user, None, title, body, _int(nav_order),\n                       \"A Page needs a title\", 400)\n    page_id = pages.create_page(conn, title, body, _int(nav_order), auth.now(request))\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/pages/{page_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{page_id}\")\ndef page_edit(page_id: int, request: Request, user=Depends(auth.require_user),\n              conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    page = _page(conn, page_id)\n    return _editor(request, user, page, page.title, page.body, page.nav_order)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{page_id}\")\ndef page_update(page_id: int, request: Request, title: str = Form(\"\"), body: str = Form(\"\"),\n                nav_order: str = Form(\"0\"), slug: str | None = Form(None),\n                user=Depends(auth.require_user), conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    page = _page(conn, page_id)\n    title = title.strip()\n    if not title:\n        return _editor(request, user, page, title, body, _int(nav_order),\n                       \"A Page needs a title\", 400)\n new_slug)\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/pages/{page_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\nCONFIRMS = {\n    \"publish\": (\"Publish this?\", \"Yes, publish it\", DRAFT, PUBLISHED,\n                \"It becomes Live at the next Publish run.\"),\n    \"unpublish\": (\"Are you sure you want to unpublish this?\", \"Yes, unpublish it\",\n                  PUBLISHED, DRAFT,\n                  \"It returns to Draft and leaves the public site at the next Publish run.\"),\n}\n\n\ndef _confirm(action: str, page_id: int, request, user, conn):\n    heading, button, _, _, note = CONFIRMS[action]\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/page_confirm.html\",\n        {\"title\": heading, \"user\": user, \"page\": _page(conn, page_id), \"action\": action,\n         \"heading\": heading, \"button\": button, \"note\": note})\n\n\ndef _change(action: str, page_id: int, request, conn):\n    _, _, from_status, to_status, _ = CONFIRMS[action]\n    if _page(conn, status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{page_id}/publish\")\ndef page_publish_confirm(page_id: int, request: Request, user=Depends(auth.require_user),\n                         conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    return _confirm(\"publish\", page_id, request, user, conn)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{page_id}/publish\")\ndef page_publish(page_id: int, request: Request, import db\nfrom app.posts import DRAFT, PUBLISHED, slugify\n\nSEED_TITLES = (\"Homepage\", \"About\", \"Leadership\", \"Analyst Program\", \"Join\", \"Recruiting\")\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass Page:\n    id: int\n    title: str\n    slug: str\n    body: str\n    status: str\n    nav_order: int\n    created_at: float\n    updated_at: float\n\n\ndef _row(row: sqlite3.Row) -> Page:\n    return Page(row[\"id\"], row[\"title\"], row[\"slug\"], row[\"body\"], row[\"status\"],\n                row[\"nav_order\"], row[\"created_at\"], row[\"updated_at\"])\n\n\ndef _unique_slug(conn: sqlite3.Connection, title: str) -> str:\n    base = slugify(title)\n    slug, n = base, 1\n    while conn.execute(\"SELECT 1 FROM pages WHERE slug = ?\", (slug,)).fetchone():\n        n += 1\n        slug = f\"{base}-{n}\"\n    return slug\n\n\ndef create_page(conn: sqlite3.Connection, title: str, body: str, nav_order: int,\n                now: float) -> int:\n    \"\"\"New Pages are always Drafts.\"\"\"\n    with conn:\n        cur = conn.execute(\n            \"INSERT INTO pages (title, slug, body, status, nav_order, created_at, updated_at)\"\n            \" VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n            (title, _unique_slug(conn, title), body, DRAFT, nav_order, now, now))\n    return cur.lastrowid\n\n\ndef get_page(conn: sqlite3.Connection, page_id: int) -> Page | None:\n    row = conn.execute(\"SELECT * FROM pages WHERE id = ?\", (page_id,)).fetchone()\n    return _row(row) if row else None\n\n\ndef list_pages(conn: sqlite3.Connection, status: str | None = None) -> list[Page]:\n    \"\"\"Newest first, optionally one status.\"\"\"\n    where, args = (\"\", ()) if status is None else (\" WHERE status = ?\", (status,))\n    rows = conn.execute(\"SELECT * FROM pages\" + where + \" ORDER BY created_at DESC, id DESC\",\n                        args).fetchall()\n    return [_row(r) for r in rows]\n\n\ndef count_by_status(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> dict[str, int]:\n    found = {r[\"status\"]: r[\"n\"] for r in\n             conn.execute(\"SELECT status, COUNT(*) AS n FROM pages GROUP BY status\")}\n    return {DRAFT: found.get(DRAFT, 0), PUBLISHED: found.get(PUBLISHED, 0)}\n\n\ndef update_page(conn: sqlite3.Connection, page_id: int, title: str, body: str,\n                nav_order: int, now: float, slug: str | None = None) -> None:\n    \"\"\"The slug changes only when a normalised `slug` is passed.\"\"\"\n    with conn:\n        conn.execute(\n            \"UPDATE pages SET title = ?, body = ?, nav_order = ?, updated_at = ?,\"\n            \" slug = COALESCE(?, slug) WHERE id = ?\",\n            (title, body, nav_order, now, slug, page_id))\n\n\ndef set_status(conn: sqlite3.Connection, page_id: int, status: str, now: float) -> None:\n    with conn:\n        conn.execute(\"UPDATE pages SET status = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?\",\n                     (status, now, page_id))\n\n\ndef slug_taken(conn: sqlite3.Connection, slug: str, page_id: int) -> bool:\n    return conn.execute(\"SELECT 1 FROM pages WHERE slug = ? AND id != ?\",\n                        (slug, page_id)).fetchone() is not None\n\n\ndef delete_page(conn: sqlite3.Connection, page_id: int) -> None:\n    with conn:\n        conn.execute(\"DELETE FROM pages WHERE id = ?\", (page_id,))\n\n\ndef seed_pages(database: Path) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"Create the club's named Pages as Drafts; existing titles are left alone.\"\"\"\n    db.init_db(database)\n    conn = db.connect(database)\n    existing = {p.title for p in list_pages(conn)}\n    created = []\n    for order, title in enumerate(SEED_TITLES, start=1):\n        if title not in existing:\n            create_page(conn, title, \"\", order, time.time())\n            created.append(title)\n    conn.close()\n    return created\nEOF\ncat > app/routes/pages.py <<'EOF'\n\"\"\"Console routes for Pages. Page:\n    id: int\n    title: str\n    slug: str\n    body: str\n    status: str\n    nav_order: int\n    created_at: float\n    updated_at: float\n\n\ndef _row(row: sqlite3.Row) -> Page:\n    return Page(row[\"id\"], row[\"title\"], row[\"slug\"], row[\"body\"], row[\"status\"],\n                row[\"nav_order\"], row[\"created_at\"], row[\"updated_at\"])\n\n\ndef _unique_slug(conn: sqlite3.Connection, title: str) -> str:\n    base = slugify(title)\n    slug, n = base, 1\n    while conn.execute(\"SELECT 1 FROM pages WHERE slug = ?\", (slug,)).fetchone():\n        n += 1\n        slug = f\"{base}-{n}\"\n    return slug\n\n\ndef create_page(conn: sqlite3.Connection, title: str, body: str, nav_order: int,\n                now: float) -> int:\n    \"\"\"New Pages are always Drafts.\"\"\"\n    with conn:\n        cur = conn.execute(\n            \"INSERT INTO pages (title, slug, body, status, nav_order, created_at, updated_at)\"\n            \" VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n            (title, _unique_slug(conn, title), body, DRAFT, nav_order, now, now))\n    return cur.lastrowid\n\n\ndef get_page(conn: sqlite3.Connection, page_id: int) -> Page | None:\n    row = conn.execute(\"SELECT * FROM pages WHERE id = ?\", (page_id,)).fetchone()\n    return _row(row) if row else None\n\n\ndef list_pages(conn: sqlite3.Connection, status: str | None = None) -> list[Page]:\n    \"\"\"Newest first, optionally one status.\"\"\"\n    where, args = (\"\", ()) if status is None else (\" WHERE status = ?\", (status,))\n    rows = conn.execute(\"SELECT * FROM pages\" + where + \" ORDER BY created_at DESC, id DESC\",\n                        args).fetchall()\n    return [_row(r) for r in rows]\n\n\ndef count_by_status(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> dict[str, int]:\n    found = {r[\"status\"]: r[\"n\"] for r in\n             conn.execute(\"SELECT status, COUNT(*) AS n FROM pages GROUP BY status\")}\n    return {DRAFT: found.get(DRAFT, 0), PUBLISHED: found.get(PUBLISHED, 0)}\n\n\ndef update_page(conn: sqlite3.Connection, page_id: int, title: str, body: str,\n                nav_order: int, now: float, slug: str | None = None) -> None:\n    \"\"\"The slug changes only when a normalised `slug` is passed.\"\"\"\n    with conn:\n        conn.execute(\n            \"UPDATE pages SET title = ?, body = ?, nav_order = ?, updated_at = ?,\"\n            \" slug = COALESCE(?, slug) WHERE id = ?\",\n            (title, body, nav_order, now, slug, page_id))\n\n\ndef set_status(conn: sqlite3.Connection, page_id: int, status: str, now: float) -> None:\n    with conn:\n        conn.execute(\"UPDATE pages SET status = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?\",\n                     (status, now, page_id))\n\n\ndef slug_taken(conn: sqlite3.Connection, slug: str, page_id: int) -> bool:\n    return conn.execute(\"SELECT 1 FROM pages WHERE slug = ? AND id != ?\",\n                        (slug, page_id)).fetchone() is not None\n\n\ndef delete_page(conn: sqlite3.Connection, page_id: int) -> None:\n    with conn:\n        conn.execute(\"DELETE FROM pages WHERE id = ?\", (page_id,))\n\n\ndef seed_pages(database: Path) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"Create the club's named Pages as Drafts; existing titles are left alone.\"\"\"\n    db.init_db(database)\n    conn = db.connect(database)\n    existing = {p.title for p in list_pages(conn)}\n    created = []\n    for order, title in enumerate(SEED_TITLES, start=1):\n        if title not in existing:\n            create_page(conn, title, \"\", order, time.time())\n            created.append(title)\n    conn.close()\n    return created\nEOF\ncat > app/routes/pages.py <<'EOF'\n\"\"\"Console routes for Pages. President-only: every route is behind the admin guard.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Form, HTTPException, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\n\nfrom app import auth, db, pages\nfrom app.markdown import render_markdown\nfrom app.posts import DRAFT, PUBLISHED, slugify\nfrom app.templating import templates\n\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/admin/pages\", dependencies=[Depends(auth.require_role(\"admin\"))])\n\n\ndef _page(conn, page_id: int) -> pages.Page:\n    page = pages.get_page(conn, page_id)\n    if page is None:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=\"No such Page\")\n    return page\n\n\ndef _int(value: str) -> int:\n    try:\n        return int(value.strip() or 0)\n    except ValueError:\n        return 0\n\n\ndef _editor(request, user, page, title, body, nav_order, error=None, status_code=200):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/page_edit.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Edit Page\" if page else \"New Page\", \"user\": user, \"page\": page,\n         \"form_title\": title, \"form_body\": body, \"form_nav_order\": nav_order,\n         \"preview_html\": render_markdown(body), \"error\": error},\n        status_code=status_code)\n\n\n@router.get(\"\")\ndef page_list():\n    return RedirectResponse(\"/admin/posts?type=page\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/new\")\ndef page_new(request: Request, user=Depends(auth.require_user)):\n    return _editor(request, user, None, \"\", \"\", 0)\n\n\n@router.post(\"\")\ndef page_create(request: Request, title: str = Form(\"\"), body: str = Form(\"\"),\n                nav_order: str = Form(\"0\"), user=Depends(auth.require_user),\n                conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    title = title.strip()\n    if not title:\n        return _editor(request, user, None, title, body, _int(nav_order),\n                       \"A Page needs a title\", 400)\n    page_id = pages.create_page(conn, title, body, _int(nav_order), auth.now(request))\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/pages/{page_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{page_id}\")\ndef page_edit(page_id: int, request: Request, user=Depends(auth.require_user),\n              conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    page = _page(conn, page_id)\n    return _editor(request, user, page, page.title, page.body, page.nav_order)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{page_id}\")\ndef page_update(page_id: int, request: Request, title: str = Form(\"\"), body: str = Form(\"\"),\n                nav_order: str = Form(\"0\"), slug: str | None = Form(None),\n                user=Depends(auth.require_user), conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    page = _page(conn, page_id)\n    title = title.strip()\n    if not title:\n        return _editor(request, user, page, title, body, _int(nav_order),\n                       \"A Page needs a title\", 400)\n    new_slug = slugify(slug) if slug and slug.strip() else None\n    if new_slug and pages.slug_taken(conn, new_slug, page_id):\n        return _editor(request, user, page, title, body, _int(nav_order),\n                       f\"The slug “{new_slug}” is already used by another Page\", 400)\n    pages.update_page(conn, page_id, title, body, _int(nav_order), auth.now(request), new_slug)\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/pages/{page_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\nCONFIRMS = {\n    \"publish\": (\"Publish this?\", \"Yes, publish it\", DRAFT, PUBLISHED,\n                \"It becomes Live at the next Publish run.\"),\n    \"unpublish\": (\"Are you sure you want to unpublish this?\", \"Yes, unpublish it\",\n                  PUBLISHED, DRAFT,\n                  \"It returns to Draft and leaves the public site at the next Publish run.\"),\n}\n\n\ndef _confirm(action: str, page_id: int, request, user, conn):\n    heading, button, _, _, note = CONFIRMS[action]\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/page_confirm.html\",\n        {\"title\": heading, \"user\": user, \"page\": _page(conn, page_id), \"action\": action,\n         \"heading\": heading, \"button\": button, \"note\": note})\n\n\ndef _change(action: str, page_id: int, request, conn):\n    _, _, from_status, to_status, _ = CONFIRMS[action]\n    if _page(conn, page_id).status == from_status:\n        pages.set_status(conn, page_id, to_status, auth.now(request))\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/pages/{page_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{page_id}/publish\")\ndef page_publish_confirm(page_id: int, request: Request, user=Depends(auth.require_user),\n                         conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    return _confirm(\"publish\", page_id, request, user, conn)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{page_id}/publish\")\ndef page_publish(page_id: int, request: Request, conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    return _change(\"publish\", page_id, request, conn)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{page_id}/unpublish\")\ndef page_unpublish_confirm(page_id: int, request: Request, user=Depends(auth.require_user),\n                           conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    return _confirm(\"unpublish\", page_id, request, user, conn)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{page_id}/unpublish\")\ndef page_unpublish(page_id: int, request: Request, conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    return _change(\"unpublish\", page_id, request, conn)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{page_id}/delete\")\ndef page_delete_confirm(page_id: int, request: Request, user=Depends(auth.require_user),\n                        conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/page_delete.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Delete Page\", \"user\": user, \"page\": _page(conn, page_id)})\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{page_id}/delete\")\ndef page_delete(page_id: int, conn=Depends(db.get_db)):\n    pages.delete_page(conn, _page(conn, page_id).id)\n    return RedirectResponse(\"/admin/posts?type=page\", status_code=303)\nEOF\ncat > templates/admin/page_edit.html <<'EOF'\n{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>{{ title }}</h1>\n  <p><a href=\"/admin/posts?type=page\">All Pages</a></p>\n  {% if error %}<p role=\"alert\">{{ error }}</p>{% endif %}\n  <form method=\"post\" action=\"{{ '/admin/pages/' ~ page.id if page else '/admin/pages' }}\">\n    <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token() }}\">\n    <p><label>Title<br>\n      <input type=\"text\" name=\"title\" value=\"{{ form_title }}\" required style=\"width: 100%;\"></label></p>\n    {% if page %}\n    <p><label>Slug<br>\n      <input type=\"text\" name=\"slug\" value=\"{{ page.slug }}\" style=\"width: 100%;\"></label></p>\n    <p>Status: {{ page.status | capitalize }}</p>\n    {% endif %}\n    <p><label>Navigation order<br>\n      <input type=\"number\" name=\"nav_order\" value=\"{{ form_nav_order }}\"></label></p>\n    <p><label>Body (Markdown)<br>\n      <textarea name=\"body\" rows=\"14\" style=\"width: 100%;\">{{ form_body }}</textarea></label></p>\n    <p><button type=\"submit\">Save</button></p>\n  </form>\n  <section aria-label=\"Preview\">\n    <h2>Preview</h2>\n    {% if not page or page.status == 'draft' %}<p><strong>Draft, not Live</strong></p>\n    {% else %}<p><strong>Published</strong> (Live after the next Publish run)</p>{% endif %}\n    {{ preview_html | safe }}\n  </section>\n  {% if page %}\n  <p>\n    {% if page.status == 'draft' %}<a href=\"/admin/pages/{{ page.id }}/publish\">Publish</a>\n    {% else %}<a href=\"/admin/pages/{{ page.id }}/unpublish\">Unpublish</a>{% endif %} ·\n    <a href=\"/admin/pages/{{ page.id }}/delete\">Delete this Page</a>\n  </p>\n  {% endif %}\n{% endblock %}\nEOF\ncat > templates/admin/page_confirm.html <<'EOF'\n{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>{{ heading }}</h1>\n  <p>“{{ page.title }}” — {{ note }}</p>\n  <form method=\"post\" action=\"/admin/pages/{{ page.id }}/{{ action }}\">\n    <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token() }}\">\n    <button type=\"submit\">{{ button }}</button>\n    <a href=\"/admin/pages/{{ page.id }}\">Cancel</a>\n  </form>\n{% endblock %}\nEOF\ncat > templates/admin/page_delete.html <<'EOF'\n{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>Delete this Page?</h1>\n  <p>Are you sure you want to permanently delete “{{ page.title }}”? This cannot be undone.</p>\n  <form method=\"post\" action=\"/admin/pages/{{ page.id }}/delete\">\n    <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token() }}\">\n    <button type=\"submit\">Yes, delete it</button>\n    <a href=\"/admin/pages/{{ page.id }}\">Cancel</a>\n  </form>\n{% endblock %}\nEOF\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}]}}
